==== BEGIN FILE src/utils/prng.ts BLOB bfb4907710672a3411e4481ccfee3cbb5ee8bbbe ====
// Deterministic Mulberry32 PRNG for auditable expedition replays and market streams
export class DeterministicRNG {
  private state: number;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.state = seed >>> 0;
  }

  public next(): number {
    let t = (this.state += 0x6d2b79f5);
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  }

  public nextInt(min: number, max: number): number {
    return Math.floor(this.next() * (max - min + 1)) + min;
  }

  public pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
    return arr[Math.floor(this.next() * arr.length)];
  }

  public getSeed(): number {
    return this.state;
  }
}

export function hashString(input: string): string {
  let h1 = 0xdeadbeef ^ input.length;
  let h2 = 0x41c6ce57 ^ input.length;
  for (let i = 0, ch; i < input.length; i++) {
    ch = input.charCodeAt(i);
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ ch, 2654435761);
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ ch, 1597334677);
  }
  h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  return (4294967296 * (2097151 & h2) + (h1 >>> 0)).toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(12, '0');
}

==== BEGIN FILE src/engine/marketContext.ts BLOB 36a560e830649dafe598d16f9786e93565720ab5 ====
import { DailyMarketContext, RegionId } from '../types/game';
import { DeterministicRNG, hashString } from '../utils/prng';
import { SIM_RULES_VERSION } from './simulation';

/** Domain-separated daily stream: independent of strategy and incidental RNG draws.
 * Reconstructible from saved inputs; contracts/financial records retain the operative context. */
export function getDailyMarketContext(seed: number, day: number, region: RegionId,
  rulesVersion = SIM_RULES_VERSION): DailyMarketContext {
  const key = `market-v1|${rulesVersion}|${seed}|${day}|${region}`;
  const rng = new DeterministicRNG(parseInt(hashString(key).slice(-8), 16));
  return { version: 'market-v1', rulesVersion, seed, day, region,
    factor: 0.85 + rng.next() * 0.37 };
}

import { GameSaveState, RegionId, ScheduledMatchup } from '../types/game';
import { REAGENTS_CATALOG, TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG, INITIAL_PRODUCTS, SKILL_TREE_NODES } from '../data/catalog';
import { INITIAL_MASTERS, INITIAL_PARTIES, INITIAL_RIVALS, INITIAL_INTEL_FEED } from '../data/world';
import { SIM_RULES_VERSION, computeCanonicalFormulaSignature } from './simulation';

function buildInitialScheduledMatchups(): Record<RegionId, ScheduledMatchup> {
  const result = {} as Record<RegionId, ScheduledMatchup>;
  (['cellar', 'warrens', 'blackspire', 'vault'] as const).forEach((reg) => {
    const m = INITIAL_MASTERS.find((x) => x.region === reg) || INITIAL_MASTERS[0];
    const p = INITIAL_PARTIES.find((x) => x.region === reg) || INITIAL_PARTIES[0];
    result[reg] = { masterId: m.id, partyId: p.id };
  });
  return result;
}

export function createInitialSaveState(seed: number = 20260001): GameSaveState {
  const initReagents: Record<string, number> = {};
  REAGENTS_CATALOG.slice(0, 20).forEach((r) => {
    initReagents[r.id] = 3;
  });
  const initParts: Record<string, number> = {};
  TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.slice(0, 20).forEach((p) => {
    initParts[p.id] = 2;
  });

  return {
    version: '2.1.0-sim002b',
    seed,
    day: 1,
    phase: 'MORNING',
    activeRegion: 'cellar',
    unlockedRegions: ['cellar'],
    gold: 1850,
    debt: 0,
    skillPoints: 2,
    customBrewsCount: 0,
    customTrapsCount: 0,
    scheduledMatchups: buildInitialScheduledMatchups(),
    reagentInventory: initReagents,
    partInventory: initParts,
    products: INITIAL_PRODUCTS.map((p) => {
      const kind = p.category === 'TRAP_MASTER' || p.category === 'HAZARD_MONSTER' ? 'trp' : 'alc';
      return {
        ...p,
        formulaSignature: computeCanonicalFormulaSignature(kind, p.recipeComponents, [], SIM_RULES_VERSION),
        rulesVersion: SIM_RULES_VERSION,
        appliedSkills: [],
        counterClasses: [...p.counterClasses],
        recipeComponents: [...p.recipeComponents],
        reactionNotes: [...p.reactionNotes]
      };
    }),
    masters: INITIAL_MASTERS.map((m) => ({
      ...m,
      knownHeroCounters: [...m.knownHeroCounters],
      installedHazards: [...m.installedHazards],
      installedResetCharges: m.installedHazards.map(() => 0),
      memoryLog: [...m.memoryLog]
    })),
    parties: INITIAL_PARTIES.map((p) => ({
      ...p,
      members: p.members.map((mem) => ({ ...mem })),
      observedTraps: [...p.observedTraps],
      equippedProducts: [...p.equippedProducts],
      memoryLog: [...p.memoryLog]
    })),
    rivals: INITIAL_RIVALS.map((r) => ({ ...r })),
    contractsToday: [],
    contractHistory: [],
    intelFeed: INITIAL_INTEL_FEED.map((i) => ({ ...i })),
    expeditionReports: [],
    financialLedger: [],
    catalystLedger: [],
    expertUnlocked: false,
    skills: SKILL_TREE_NODES.map((s) => ({ ...s })),
    basiliskChainStage: 1,
    blackspireResolution: 'NONE',
    rulesVersion: SIM_RULES_VERSION,
    tradeTerms: { priceMultiplier: 1, warrantyBacked: false, exclusiveDeal: false, secretProvenance: false },
    insolvencyEvents: 0
  };
}

==== BEGIN FILE src/engine/progression.ts BLOB 7b46bf83680fcf23916f07d515ba3f21cb6a2bf1 ====
import { GameSaveState } from '../types/game';
import { hashString } from '../utils/prng';
export function canClaimSoloCatalyst(s: GameSaveState): boolean {
  return !s.expertUnlocked && s.unlockedRegions.includes('blackspire') && s.customBrewsCount >= 2 && s.customTrapsCount >= 2
    && s.contractHistory.filter(c=>c.buyerType==='MASTER').length >= 2
    && s.contractHistory.filter(c=>c.buyerType==='HERO').length >= 2;
}
export function claimSoloCatalyst(s: GameSaveState): GameSaveState {
  if (!canClaimSoloCatalyst(s)) return s;
  const hash=hashString(`SOLO_VESPERA_CATALYST_DAY_${s.day}_${s.seed}`).slice(0,8).toUpperCase();
  return { ...s, expertUnlocked:true, activeRegion:'vault',
    unlockedRegions:s.unlockedRegions.includes('vault') ? s.unlockedRegions : [...s.unlockedRegions,'vault'],
    catalystLedger:[{id:`cat_solo_d${s.day}_${hash.toLowerCase()}`,senderName:'Arch-Architect Vespera (Blackspire Panopticon)',
      senderType:'SOLO_DM_COURIER',provenanceHash:hash,dayReceived:s.day,
      commissionNote:'Solo Offline Commission Fulfilled in Blackspire: Demonstrated custom offensive trap engineering, stabilised alchemy, and bilateral double-sales.',
      isAuthentic:true},...s.catalystLedger] };
}

==== BEGIN FILE src/engine/economyEngine.ts BLOB 12af2509a510c4c83e21913057b25fa994d4f9b6 ====
import { getDailyMarketContext } from './marketContext';
import {
  GameSaveState,
  CraftedProduct,
  DungeonMaster,
  AdventuringParty,
  SaleContract,
  RegionId,
  CounterClass
} from '../types/game';
import { DeterministicRNG } from '../utils/prng';

export interface QuotationOptions {
  priceMultiplier?: number; // e.g. 1.04, 1.22
  warrantyBacked: boolean;
  exclusiveDeal: boolean;
  secretProvenance: boolean;
  day: number;
  seed: number;
  unlockedSkills?: string[];
  rulesVersion?: string;
}

export interface QuotationResult {
  unitPrice: number;
  baseMultiplier: number;
  trustFactor: number;
  qualityPremium: number;
  boycotting: boolean;
  canAfford: boolean;
  acceptable: boolean;
  refusalReason?: string;
  expectedTrustDelta: number;
}

export interface TransactionResult {
  success: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  contract?: SaleContract;
  unitPrice?: number;
  nextState?: GameSaveState;
}

/**
 * Deterministic customer boycott evaluation.
 * Triggered when client trust < 50; chance scales with severity of trust deficit.
 */
export function evaluateCustomerBoycott(
  buyer: DungeonMaster | AdventuringParty,
  day: number,
  seed: number
): boolean {
  if (buyer.trust >= 50) return false;
  // Deterministic seed incorporating buyer id
  let hashVal = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < buyer.id.length; i++) {
    hashVal = (hashVal * 31 + buyer.id.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  }
  const rng = new DeterministicRNG(seed + day * 104729 + hashVal);
  const boycottChance = (54 - buyer.trust) * 0.016;
  return rng.next() < boycottChance;
}

/**
 * Authoritative shared commercial quotation calculation.
 * Used by BOTH player UI (TradeDesk) and headless campaign runner.
 */
export function computeQuotation(
  product: CraftedProduct,
  buyer: DungeonMaster | AdventuringParty,
  buyerType: 'MASTER' | 'HERO',
  options: QuotationOptions
): QuotationResult {
  const priceMultiplier = options.priceMultiplier ?? 1.0;
  const marketSentiment = getDailyMarketContext(options.seed, options.day, buyer.region, options.rulesVersion).factor;
  const skills = options.unlockedSkills ?? [];
  const hasSkill = (k: string) => skills.includes(k);

  // 1. Base Multiplier from price setting, contract options, and market sentiment
  const hasBilateral = hasSkill('bilateral_margin');
  const baseMultiplier =
    priceMultiplier *
    (options.warrantyBacked ? 1.12 : 1.0) *
    (options.exclusiveDeal ? 1.22 : 1.0) *
    (hasBilateral ? 1.1 : 1.0) *
    marketSentiment;

  // 2. Buyer Trust Factor:
  // For trust < 55: scales down (at trust 48: 0.78 + 48*0.003 = 0.924)
  // For trust >= 85: loyalty premium (1.08 for master, 1.09 for hero)
  let trustFactor = 1.0;
  if (buyer.trust < 55) {
    trustFactor = 0.78 + buyer.trust * 0.003;
  } else if (buyer.trust >= 85) {
    trustFactor = buyerType === 'HERO' ? 1.09 : 1.08;
  }

  // 3. Product Quality Premium (High-purity / High-stability R&D)
  let qualityPremium = 1.0;
  if (buyerType === 'HERO' && product.stability >= 75) {
    if (hasSkill('paradox_alchemy')) {
      qualityPremium = 1.24;
    } else if (hasSkill('chelate_boost')) {
      qualityPremium = 1.15;
    }
  }

  // 4. Secret provenance cost offset (-35g for removing maker's marks)
  const secretOffset = options.secretProvenance ? 35 : 0;

  // Final Unit Price: floor clamped to 25g
  const rawQuotedPrice = Math.round(
    product.suggestedPrice * baseMultiplier * trustFactor * qualityPremium
  );
  const unitPrice = Math.max(25, rawQuotedPrice - secretOffset);

  // 5. Customer Acceptance & Refusal Rules
  const boycotting = evaluateCustomerBoycott(buyer, options.day, options.seed);
  const canAfford = buyer.budget >= unitPrice;

  let acceptable = true;
  let refusalReason: string | undefined;

  const capacity = buyerType === 'MASTER' ? 3 : 4;
  const loadout = buyerType === 'MASTER' ? (buyer as DungeonMaster).installedHazards : (buyer as AdventuringParty).equippedProducts;
  if (loadout.length >= capacity) {
    acceptable = false;
    refusalReason = `Loadout full (${loadout.length}/${capacity}). Use existing supplies before purchasing another unit.`;
  } else if (boycotting) {
    acceptable = false;
    refusalReason = `Client boycott in effect due to low trust (${buyer.trust}/100).`;
  } else if (!canAfford) {
    acceptable = false;
    refusalReason = `Insufficient budget: Client has ${buyer.budget}g but item costs ${unitPrice}g.`;
  } else if (buyer.trust < 50 && priceMultiplier > 1.15) {
    // Low-trust clients refuse price-gouging markups
    acceptable = false;
    refusalReason = `Client refuses price gouging (${Math.round((priceMultiplier - 1) * 100)}% markup) while trust is below 50.`;
  }

  // 6. Expected Trust Delta on transaction
  let expectedTrustDelta = 0;
  if (priceMultiplier >= 1.2) {
    expectedTrustDelta -= 3;
  } else if (options.warrantyBacked) {
    expectedTrustDelta += buyerType === 'HERO' ? 6 : 5;
  } else {
    expectedTrustDelta += buyerType === 'HERO' ? 3 : 2;
  }

  return {
    unitPrice,
    baseMultiplier,
    trustFactor,
    qualityPremium,
    boycotting,
    canAfford,
    acceptable,
    refusalReason,
    expectedTrustDelta
  };
}

/**
 * Authoritative shared trade transaction execution.
 * Mutates state immutably and returns updated GameSaveState.
 */
export function executeCommercialTransaction(
  prevState: GameSaveState,
  buyerType: 'MASTER' | 'HERO',
  buyerId: string,
  product: CraftedProduct,
  terms: {
    priceMultiplier?: number;
    warrantyBacked: boolean;
    exclusiveDeal: boolean;
    secretProvenance: boolean;
    rulesVersion?: string;
  }
): TransactionResult {
  if (!terms || !Number.isFinite(terms.priceMultiplier ?? 1) || (terms.priceMultiplier ?? 1) <= 0
      || !(['warrantyBacked','exclusiveDeal','secretProvenance'] as const).every(k => typeof terms[k] === 'boolean')) {
    return { success:false, reason:'Invalid commercial terms.' };
  }
  // 1. Strict validation: check current authoritative product stock (Gate 3)
  if (!product || !product.id || !Array.isArray(prevState.products)) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Invalid or missing product reference.' };
  }
  const currentProduct = prevState.products.find((p) => p && p.id === product.id);
  if (!currentProduct || !Number.isSafeInteger(currentProduct.stock) || currentProduct.stock <= 0) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Product is out of stock or reference is stale.' };
  }

  // 2. Strict validation: target audience match
  if (buyerType === 'MASTER' && currentProduct.targetAudience === 'HERO') {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Product is formulated exclusively for hero parties.' };
  }
  if (buyerType === 'HERO' && currentProduct.targetAudience === 'MASTER') {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Trap hazard is engineered exclusively for dungeon masters.' };
  }

  // 3. Strict validation: buyer existence & regional accessibility
  const buyer =
    buyerType === 'MASTER'
      ? prevState.masters.find((m) => m.id === buyerId)
      : prevState.parties.find((p) => p.id === buyerId);

  if (!buyer) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Buyer not found in region.' };
  }

  if (
    buyer.region !== prevState.activeRegion
  ) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Trading is regional: select the buyer’s unlocked region before selling.' };
  }

  const loadout = buyerType === 'MASTER'
    ? (buyer as DungeonMaster).installedHazards : (buyer as AdventuringParty).equippedProducts;
  const capacity = buyerType === 'MASTER' ? 3 : 4;
  if (loadout.length >= capacity) return { success: false,
    reason: `Loadout full (${loadout.length}/${capacity}). Use existing supplies before purchasing another unit.` };
  if (!prevState.unlockedRegions.includes(prevState.activeRegion)) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'Active trading region is locked.' };
  }

  const skills = prevState.skills.filter((s) => s.unlocked).map((s) => s.effectKey);
  const quotation = computeQuotation(currentProduct, buyer, buyerType, {
    priceMultiplier: terms.priceMultiplier,
    warrantyBacked: terms.warrantyBacked,
    exclusiveDeal: terms.exclusiveDeal,
    secretProvenance: terms.secretProvenance,
    day: prevState.day,
    seed: prevState.seed,
    unlockedSkills: skills,
    rulesVersion: prevState.rulesVersion
  });

  if (!quotation.acceptable) {
    return { success: false, reason: quotation.refusalReason };
  }

  const unitPrice = quotation.unitPrice;
  const contractSeq = prevState.contractHistory.length + 1;
  const newContract: SaleContract = {
    marketContext: getDailyMarketContext(prevState.seed, prevState.day, prevState.activeRegion, prevState.rulesVersion),
    id: `cnt_d${prevState.day}_n${contractSeq}_${currentProduct.id}`,
    day: prevState.day,
    buyerType,
    buyerId,
    buyerName: buyer.name,
    productId: currentProduct.id,
    productName: currentProduct.name,
    quantity: 1,
    unitPrice,
    totalRevenue: unitPrice,
    unitCost: currentProduct.productionCost,
    warrantyBacked: terms.warrantyBacked,
    exclusiveDeal: terms.exclusiveDeal,
    secretProvenance: terms.secretProvenance,
    creditFinanced: false
  };

  // Decrement product stock
  const nextProducts = prevState.products.map((p) =>
    p.id === currentProduct.id ? { ...p, stock: Math.max(0, p.stock - 1) } : p
  );

  // Update Master: installed hazards (max 3), budget, trust, memory
  const nextMasters = prevState.masters.map((m) => {
    if (buyerType === 'MASTER' && m.id === buyerId) {
      return {
        ...m,
        budget: Math.max(0, m.budget - unitPrice),
        trust: Math.max(10, Math.min(100, m.trust + quotation.expectedTrustDelta)),
        installedHazards: [currentProduct.id, ...m.installedHazards],
        installedResetCharges: [currentProduct.recipeComponents.includes('part_rst_09') ? 1 : 0, ...(m.installedResetCharges ?? m.installedHazards.map(() => 0))],
        memoryLog: [
          ...m.memoryLog,
          `Day ${prevState.day}: Purchased ${currentProduct.name} [#${currentProduct.formulaSignature || currentProduct.id.slice(-6)}] for ${unitPrice}g.`
        ]
      };
    }
    return m;
  });

  // Update Party: potion charges are finite individual consumable doses (Gate 3)
  // Two purchased potions represent two distinct charges, NOT collapsed into 1 unique ID
  const nextParties = prevState.parties.map((p) => {
    if (buyerType === 'HERO' && p.id === buyerId) {
      return {
        ...p,
        budget: Math.max(0, p.budget - unitPrice),
        trust: Math.max(10, Math.min(100, p.trust + quotation.expectedTrustDelta)),
        equippedProducts: [currentProduct.id, ...p.equippedProducts],
        memoryLog: [
          ...p.memoryLog,
          `Day ${prevState.day}: Purchased ${currentProduct.name} [#${currentProduct.formulaSignature || currentProduct.id.slice(-6)}] for ${unitPrice}g.`
        ]
      };
    }
    return p;
  });

  const nextState: GameSaveState = {
    ...prevState,
    phase: 'TRADE',
    scheduledMatchups: { ...prevState.scheduledMatchups, [prevState.activeRegion]: {
      ...prevState.scheduledMatchups[prevState.activeRegion],
      ...(buyerType === 'MASTER' ? { masterId:buyerId } : { partyId:buyerId }) } },
    gold: prevState.gold + unitPrice,
    products: nextProducts,
    masters: nextMasters,
    parties: nextParties,
    contractsToday: [...prevState.contractsToday, newContract],
    contractHistory: [...prevState.contractHistory, newContract]
  };

  return {
    success: true,
    contract: newContract,
    unitPrice,
    nextState
  };
}

/**
 * Finite consumables and wear resolution (Fixes Codex A03).
 * Potions are single-use consumable charges: consuming a loadout item reduces the loadout,
 * including down to 0 charges ([]). Traps wear out without auto-reset / temporal rewind.
 */
export function resolveConsumableWear(
  party: AdventuringParty,
  master: DungeonMaster,
  warrantyClaimsTriggered: number,
  day: number,
  skills: string[]
): {
  nextEquipped: string[];
  nextInstalled: string[];
  nextResetCharges: number[];
  rewindsSpent: number;
} {
  const autoResetSkill = skills.includes('auto_reset_cam');

  // Party potion loadout: consumable charges deplete after each expedition
  // If party has potions equipped, at least 1 potion is consumed on the delve.
  // When down to 1 potion, it consumes down to []!
  let nextEquipped = [...party.equippedProducts];
  if (nextEquipped.length > 0) {
    // Consume the oldest equipped potion
    nextEquipped = nextEquipped.slice(1);
  }

  // Master trap wear: non-resetting traps exhaust their armed tension / durability
  // unless auto-reset skill or temporal rewind loop is armed
  let nextInstalled = [...master.installedHazards];
  let nextResetCharges = [...(master.installedResetCharges ?? master.installedHazards.map(() => 0))];
  let rewindsSpent = 0;
  if (!autoResetSkill) {
    // Every 3 delves (or if warranty claim / breakdown occurred), oldest trap exhausts tension
    if (day % 3 === 0 || warrantyClaimsTriggered > 0) {
      if (nextInstalled.length > 0) {
        if ((nextResetCharges[0] ?? 0) > 0) {
          nextResetCharges[0] -= 1;
          rewindsSpent = 1;
        } else {
          nextInstalled = nextInstalled.slice(1);
          nextResetCharges = nextResetCharges.slice(1);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    nextEquipped,
    nextInstalled,
    nextResetCharges,
    rewindsSpent
  };
}

/**
 * Regional Exclusivity Breach and Warranty Settlement.
 */
export function computeRegionalFinesAndClaims(
  regionId: RegionId,
  exclusivityBreachDetected: boolean,
  warrantyClaimsTriggered: number,
  skills: string[]
): {
  breachFine: number;
  warrantyPayout: number;
  totalClaimsAndFines: number;
} {
  const breachFineByRegion: Record<RegionId, number> = {
    cellar: 160,
    warrens: 240,
    blackspire: 380,
    vault: 520
  };

  const hasWarrantyShield = skills.includes('warranty_shield');
  const claimBase = 165 * (hasWarrantyShield ? 0.6 : 1.0);

  const breachFine = exclusivityBreachDetected ? breachFineByRegion[regionId] : 0;
  const warrantyPayout = Math.round(warrantyClaimsTriggered * claimBase);

  return {
    breachFine,
    warrantyPayout,
    totalClaimsAndFines: breachFine + warrantyPayout
  };
}

import { GameSaveState } from '../types/game';
import { INITIAL_PRODUCTS } from '../data/catalog';

export const SAVE_BACKUP_KEY = 'trap_and_tonic_save_v2_backup';
export const HISTORICAL_V2_BACKUP_KEY = 'trap_and_tonic_backup_v2';
export const LEGACY_BACKUP_KEY = 'trap_and_tonic_save_v1_backup';
export const LEGACY_SAVE_KEY = 'trap_and_tonic_save_v1';

const SOURCES = [[SAVE_STORAGE_KEY, 'PRIMARY'], [SAVE_BACKUP_KEY, 'BACKUP'],
  [HISTORICAL_V2_BACKUP_KEY, 'HISTORICAL_V2_BACKUP'], [LEGACY_BACKUP_KEY, 'LEGACY_BACKUP'],

const regions = ['cellar', 'warrens', 'blackspire', 'vault'];
function ensure(ok: unknown, path: string): asserts ok { if (!ok) throw new Error(`Invalid save: ${path}`); }
function object(x: any) { return x !== null && typeof x === 'object' && !Array.isArray(x); }
function finite(x: any) { return typeof x === 'number' && Number.isFinite(x); }
function count(x: any) { return Number.isSafeInteger(x) && x >= 0; }
function strings(x: any) { return Array.isArray(x) && x.every(v => typeof v === 'string'); }
function fields(x: any, template: any, path: string) {
  ensure(object(x), path);
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(template)) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) ensure(Array.isArray(x[k]), `${path}.${k}`);
    else if (typeof v === 'number') ensure(finite(x[k]), `${path}.${k}`);
    else ensure(typeof x[k] === typeof v && x[k] !== null, `${path}.${k}`);
  }
}
function unique(xs: any[], path: string) {
  ensure(new Set(xs.map(x => x.id)).size === xs.length, `${path}: duplicate IDs`);
}

/** Only explicitly versioned additions are defaulted. Damaged progress/stat fields are never guessed. */
export function normalizeLoadedSave(input: Partial<GameSaveState>): GameSaveState {
  const s = structuredClone(input) as GameSaveState;
  ensure(object(s) && ['1.0.0', '2.0.0-sim002', '2.1.0-sim002b'].includes(s.version), 'unsupported persistence version');
  const legacy = s.version !== '2.1.0-sim002b';
  if (legacy) {
    s.customBrewsCount ??= 0; s.customTrapsCount ??= 0; s.insolvencyEvents ??= 0;
    s.rulesVersion ??= 'v2.0-sim002';
    s.tradeTerms ??= { priceMultiplier: 1, warrantyBacked: false, exclusiveDeal: false, secretProvenance: false };
    // Older UI omitted the ninth catalogue entry despite already installing it. Restore only its definition, no shop stock.
    if (Array.isArray(s.products) && Array.isArray(s.masters) && Array.isArray(s.parties)) {
      const refs = [...s.masters.flatMap(m => m?.installedHazards ?? []), ...s.parties.flatMap(p => p?.equippedProducts ?? [])];
      for (const id of new Set(refs)) if (!s.products.some(p => p?.id === id)) {
        const known = INITIAL_PRODUCTS.find(p => p.id === id);
        if (known) s.products.push({ ...structuredClone(known), stock: 0 });
      }
    }
    for (const m of s.masters ?? []) if (m && Array.isArray(m.installedHazards)) m.installedResetCharges ??= m.installedHazards.map(() => 0);
    const sched: any = s.scheduledMatchups ?? {};
    for (const r of regions) {
      const m = s.masters?.find(m => m?.id === sched[r]?.masterId && m.region === r) ?? s.masters?.find(m => m?.region === r);
      const p = s.parties?.find(p => p?.id === sched[r]?.partyId && p.region === r) ?? s.parties?.find(p => p?.region === r);
      sched[r] = { masterId: m?.id, partyId: p?.id };
    }
    s.scheduledMatchups = sched;
  }
  validateState(s);
  s.version = '2.1.0-sim002b';
  return s;
}

function validateState(s: GameSaveState) {
  const base = createInitialSaveState(s.seed);
  fields(s, base, 'state');
  ensure(count(s.seed) && count(s.day) && s.day > 0, 'seed/day');
  ensure(finite(s.gold) && finite(s.debt) && s.gold >= 0 && s.debt >= 0, 'cash/debt');
  for (const k of ['skillPoints','customBrewsCount','customTrapsCount','basiliskChainStage','insolvencyEvents'] as const) ensure(count(s[k]), k);
  ensure(s.rulesVersion === 'v2.0-sim002', 'rulesVersion');
  ensure(['MORNING','PREPARATION','TRADE','EXPEDITIONS','EVENING'].includes(s.phase), 'phase');
  ensure(strings(s.unlockedRegions) && s.unlockedRegions.length > 0 && s.unlockedRegions.every(r => regions.includes(r)) && s.unlockedRegions.includes(s.activeRegion), 'regions');
  ensure(['NONE','HONEST_BROKER','CARTEL_SYNDICATE','PARTISAN_ARMOURER','CALCULATED_CHAOS'].includes(s.blackspireResolution), 'resolution');
  for (const k of ['reagentInventory','partInventory'] as const) {
    ensure(object(s[k]) && Object.entries(s[k]).every(([id,n]) => id.length > 0 && count(n)), k);
  }
  ensure(Array.isArray(s.products) && s.products.length > 0, 'products');
  for (const p of s.products) {
    // Formula metadata is optional in historical products; every gameplay stat is mandatory.
    const { formulaSignature, rulesVersion, appliedSkills, ...template } = base.products[0];
    fields(p, template, 'product');
    ensure(p.id.length > 0 && count(p.stock) && p.productionCost >= 0 && p.suggestedPrice >= 0, 'product counters');
    ensure(['HERO','MASTER','BOTH'].includes(p.targetAudience), 'product audience');
    ensure(['TONIC_HERO','COUNTER_GEAR','TRAP_MASTER','HAZARD_MONSTER','DUAL_SOLVENT'].includes(p.category), 'product category');
    for (const k of ['counterClasses','recipeComponents','reactionNotes'] as const) ensure(strings(p[k]), `product.${k}`);
    ensure(p.counterClasses.every(c => ['detection','disruption','resistance','bypass_mass','sabotage','routing','filtration'].includes(c)), 'counter classes');
    for (const k of ['formulaSignature','rulesVersion'] as const) if (p[k] !== undefined) ensure(typeof p[k] === 'string', `product.${k}`);
    if (p.appliedSkills !== undefined) ensure(strings(p.appliedSkills), 'applied skills');
    for (const k of ['potency','stability','reliability','detectionDC'] as const) ensure(p[k] >= 0 && p[k] <= 100, `product.${k}`);
  }
  unique(s.products, 'products');
  const sku = new Set(s.products.map(p => p.id));
  for (const [list, template, kind] of [[s.masters, base.masters[0], 'master'], [s.parties, base.parties[0], 'party']] as const) {
    ensure(Array.isArray(list) && list.length > 0, kind);
    for (const c of list) {
      fields(c, template, kind);
      ensure(regions.includes(c.region) && c.budget >= 0 && c.trust >= 0 && c.trust <= 100, `${kind} budget/trust/region`);
      ensure(strings(c.memoryLog), `${kind} memory`);
      if ('members' in c) {
        ensure(c.members.length > 0, 'party members');
        for (const m of c.members) {
          fields(m, base.parties[0].members[0], 'member');
          ensure(m.massKg > 0 && m.maxHp > 0 && m.hp >= 0 && m.hp <= m.maxHp, 'member stats');
          ensure(['Vanguard','Alchemist','Scout','Arcanist'].includes(m.role) && ['Healthy','Petrified','Poisoned','Recovering'].includes(m.status), 'member enums');
        }
        ensure(strings(c.observedTraps) && strings(c.equippedProducts) && c.equippedProducts.every(id => sku.has(id)), 'party SKU references');
      } else {
        ensure(strings(c.knownHeroCounters) && strings(c.installedHazards) && c.installedHazards.every(id => sku.has(id)), 'master SKU references');
        ensure(count(c.catalystCommissionStage), 'commission stage');
        ensure(Array.isArray(c.installedResetCharges) && c.installedResetCharges.length === c.installedHazards.length && c.installedResetCharges.every((n,i) => count(n) && n <= 1 && (n === 0 || s.products.find(p=>p.id===c.installedHazards[i])?.recipeComponents.includes('part_rst_09'))), 'temporal slot charges');
      }
    }
    unique(list as any[], kind);
  }
  ensure(object(s.scheduledMatchups), 'schedule');
  for (const r of regions) {
    const match = s.scheduledMatchups[r as keyof typeof s.scheduledMatchups];
    ensure(match && s.masters.some(m => m.id === match.masterId && m.region === r) && s.parties.some(p => p.id === match.partyId && p.region === r), `schedule.${r}`);
  }
  for (const skill of s.skills) {
    const known = base.skills.find(k => k.id === skill?.id);
    ensure(known, 'unknown skill'); fields(skill, known, 'skill');
    ensure(skill.effectKey === known.effectKey && skill.costPoints === known.costPoints, 'skill identity');
  }
  unique(s.skills,'skills'); ensure(s.skills.length === base.skills.length, 'missing skills');
  for (const r of s.rivals) fields(r, base.rivals[0], 'rival');
  for (const i of s.intelFeed) { fields(i, base.intelFeed[0], 'intel'); ensure(count(i.day) && regions.includes(i.region), 'intel day/region'); }
  const contract = { id:'',day:1,buyerType:'',buyerId:'',buyerName:'',productId:'',productName:'',quantity:1,unitPrice:0,totalRevenue:0,unitCost:0,warrantyBacked:false,exclusiveDeal:false,secretProvenance:false,creditFinanced:false };
  for (const xs of [s.contractsToday,s.contractHistory]) {
    for (const c of xs) {
      fields(c,contract,'contract'); ensure(count(c.day) && c.day > 0 && count(c.quantity) && c.quantity > 0 && c.unitPrice >= 0 && c.unitCost >= 0 && c.totalRevenue === c.quantity*c.unitPrice, 'contract values');
      ensure(sku.has(c.productId) && (c.buyerType === 'MASTER' ? s.masters : c.buyerType === 'HERO' ? s.parties : []).some(b => b.id === c.buyerId), 'contract references');
      if (c.marketContext) market(c.marketContext);
    } unique(xs,'contracts');
  }
  ensure(s.contractsToday.every(c => s.contractHistory.some(h => JSON.stringify(c) === JSON.stringify(h))), 'daily contract history');
  const financial = { day:1,grossRevenue:0,cogs:0,grossMargin:0,overheadAndStorage:0,warrantyAndClaims:0,finesAndBribes:0,financingInterest:0,netProfit:0,endingCash:0,inventoryValue:0,debt:0,netWorth:0 };
  for (const f of s.financialLedger) { fields(f,financial,'financial'); ensure(count(f.day) && f.endingCash >= 0 && f.debt >= 0, 'financial counters'); if (f.marketContext) market(f.marketContext); }
  for (const c of s.catalystLedger) { fields(c,{id:'',senderName:'',senderType:'',provenanceHash:'',dayReceived:1,commissionNote:'',isAuthentic:false},'catalyst'); ensure(['SOLO_DM_COURIER','PEER_QR_EXCHANGE'].includes(c.senderType) && count(c.dayReceived), 'catalyst type/day'); ensure(c.senderType !== 'PEER_QR_EXCHANGE' || !c.isAuthentic, 'community code authenticity'); }
  for (const r of s.expeditionReports) {
    fields(r,{id:'',day:1,seed:1,rulesVersion:'',region:'',masterId:'',masterName:'',partyId:'',partyName:'',masterSupplies:[],heroSupplies:[],rooms:[],totalLooted:0,masterTreasuryDelta:0,partyBudgetDelta:0,warrantyClaimsTriggered:0,exclusivityBreachDetected:false,doubleSaleDemandCreated:[],summaryHeadline:''},'report');
    ensure(s.masters.some(m=>m.id===r.masterId) && s.parties.some(p=>p.id===r.partyId) && r.rooms.length>0, 'report references');
    for (const k of ['masterSupplies','heroSupplies','doubleSaleDemandCreated'] as const) ensure(strings(r[k]), `report.${k}`);
    for (const room of r.rooms) {
      fields(room,{roomIndex:1,roomName:'',hazardName:'',hazardId:'',triggerType:'',triggerMassThresholdKg:0,heroEffectiveMassKg:1,outcome:'',damageDealt:0,lootSecured:0,causalExplanation:'',learnedByHeroes:'',learnedByMaster:''},'room');
      for (const k of ['heroCounterUsed','chemicalReaction','casualtyName'] as const) ensure(room[k] === null || typeof room[k] === 'string', `room.${k}`);
    }
  }
  fields(s.tradeTerms, base.tradeTerms, 'trade terms'); ensure(s.tradeTerms!.priceMultiplier > 0, 'trade price');
}
function market(m: any) {
  fields(m,{version:'',rulesVersion:'',seed:1,day:1,region:'',factor:1},'market');
  ensure(m.version === 'market-v1' && m.factor >= .85 && m.factor < 1.22 && regions.includes(m.region), 'market context');
}
export function validateSavePayload(raw: string | null | undefined): SaveValidationResult {
  try { ensure(typeof raw === 'string' && raw.length > 0, 'empty data');
    return { valid: true, state: normalizeLoadedSave(JSON.parse(raw)) };
  } catch (err) { return { valid: false, error: String(err) }; }
}
export function quarantinePayload(storage: Storage, raw: string) {
  const key = `${QUARANTINE_KEY}:${hashString(raw)}`;
  if (!storage.getItem(key)) storage.setItem(key, raw);
  if (!storage.getItem(QUARANTINE_KEY)) storage.setItem(QUARANTINE_KEY, raw);
}
export function loadGameWithRecovery(storage: Storage = localStorage): SaveLoadResult {
  const original = Object.fromEntries(SOURCES.map(([key]) => [key, storage.getItem(key)]));
  let rejected = false; let archiveFailed = false;
  for (const [key, source] of SOURCES) {
    const raw = original[key]; if (!raw) continue;
    const check = validateSavePayload(raw);
    if (!check.valid) { rejected = true; try { quarantinePayload(storage,raw); } catch { archiveFailed = true; } continue; }
    const migrated = JSON.stringify(check.state) !== raw;
    // Archive original storage before changing any checkpoint. On quota failure leave all slots untouched.
    if (migrated || source !== 'PRIMARY') {
      try { const snapshot = JSON.stringify(original); const id = `trap_and_tonic_pre_migration:${hashString(snapshot)}`;
        if (!storage.getItem(id)) storage.setItem(id,snapshot);
      } catch { archiveFailed = true; }
    }
    return { state: check.state!, source, recovered: source !== 'PRIMARY' || migrated,
      writeBlocked: archiveFailed,
      notice: source !== 'PRIMARY' || migrated || rejected || archiveFailed
        ? `Recovered ${key}: Day ${check.state!.day}, ${check.state!.gold}g. Original checkpoints preserved.${archiveFailed ? ' Storage full: autosave paused; export your ledger.' : ''}` : undefined };
  }
  return { state: createInitialSaveState(), source:'FRESH', recovered:false, writeBlocked:rejected,
    notice: rejected ? 'No trustworthy checkpoint found. Original saves quarantined; autosave paused. Import a valid ledger or explicitly start a new campaign.' : undefined };
}
export function persistAtomicSaveSafe(state: GameSaveState, storage: Storage = localStorage): boolean {
  try {
    const outgoing = validateSavePayload(JSON.stringify(state));
    if (!outgoing.valid) return false;
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(outgoing.state);
    const previous = storage.getItem(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (previous === serialized) return true;
    if (previous && validateSavePayload(previous).valid) storage.setItem(SAVE_BACKUP_KEY,previous);
    storage.setItem(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY,serialized);
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

import {
  Reagent,
  TrapPart,
  CraftedProduct,
  DungeonMaster,
  AdventuringParty,
  ExpeditionReport,
  ExpeditionRoomEvent,
  CounterClass,
  SaleContract,
  ParadoxCatalystEntry,
  RegionId,
  ScheduledMatchup
} from '../types/game';
import { DeterministicRNG, hashString } from '../utils/prng';

export const SIM_RULES_VERSION = 'v2.0-sim002';

/** Shared workshop/live predicate; inversion sensors detect only featherweight intruders. */
export function isMassInversionPlate(hazard: CraftedProduct): boolean {
  return hazard.recipeComponents.includes('part_trg_12');
}
export function isTrapTriggeredByMass(hazard: CraftedProduct, massKg: number): boolean {
  return isMassInversionPlate(hazard) ? massKg < 25
    : hazard.triggerMassThresholdKg <= 0 || massKg >= hazard.triggerMassThresholdKg;
}

export function computeCanonicalFormulaSignature(
  kind: 'alc' | 'trp',
  componentIds: string[],
  appliedSkills: string[] = [],
  rulesVersion: string = SIM_RULES_VERSION
): string {
  const sortedSkills = [...appliedSkills].sort().join(',');
  const payload = `${kind}|${componentIds.join(',')}|${sortedSkills}|${rulesVersion}`;
  return hashString(payload).slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
}

export function computeCanonicalProductId(
  kind: 'alc' | 'trp',
  componentIds: string[],
  appliedSkills: string[] = [],
  rulesVersion: string = SIM_RULES_VERSION
): string {
  const sig = computeCanonicalFormulaSignature(kind, componentIds, appliedSkills, rulesVersion);
  return `prod_${kind}_${sig.toLowerCase()}`;
}

/**
 * F02 Fix: Merges a newly crafted product into inventory strictly by canonical formulation identity
 * (complete component tuple + applicable skills + rulesVersion), NEVER by display name alone.
 * Distinct formulations retain distinct properties and IDs even when given identical names.
 */
export function mergeCraftedProductIntoInventory(
  existingProducts: CraftedProduct[],
  crafted: CraftedProduct,
  batchSize: number
): CraftedProduct[] {
  const craftedCompKey = crafted.recipeComponents.join(',');
  const craftedSkillsKey = [...(crafted.appliedSkills || [])].sort().join(',');
  const craftedRules = crafted.rulesVersion || SIM_RULES_VERSION;

  const existingIdx = existingProducts.findIndex((p) => {
    if (p.id === crafted.id) return true;
    const pCompKey = p.recipeComponents.join(',');
    const pSkillsKey = [...(p.appliedSkills || [])].sort().join(',');
    const pRules = p.rulesVersion || SIM_RULES_VERSION;
    return pCompKey === craftedCompKey && pSkillsKey === craftedSkillsKey && pRules === craftedRules && p.name === crafted.name;
  });

  if (existingIdx >= 0) {
    const next = [...existingProducts];
    next[existingIdx] = {
      ...crafted,
      id: next[existingIdx].id, // Preserve existing canonical/initial ID if exact same formula & name
      stock: next[existingIdx].stock + batchSize
    };
    return next;
  }

  return [{ ...crafted, stock: batchSize }, ...existingProducts];
}

/**
 * F01 Fix: Returns the explicitly scheduled Dungeon Master and Adventuring Party for a region,
 * plus the next rotation pairing so the player always knows who is participating before and after supplying.
 */
export function getRegionEncounterSchedule(
  region: RegionId,
  day: number,
  masters: DungeonMaster[],
  parties: AdventuringParty[],
  scheduledMatchups?: Record<RegionId, ScheduledMatchup>
): {
  currentMaster: DungeonMaster;
  currentParty: AdventuringParty;
  nextMaster: DungeonMaster;
  nextParty: AdventuringParty;
} {
  const regionMasters = masters.filter((m) => m.region === region);
  const regionParties = parties.filter((p) => p.region === region);

  const fallbackMaster = regionMasters[(day - 1) % Math.max(1, regionMasters.length)] || masters[0];
  const fallbackParty = regionParties[(day - 1) % Math.max(1, regionParties.length)] || parties[0];

  const scheduled = scheduledMatchups?.[region];
  const currentMaster =
    (scheduled && regionMasters.find((m) => m.id === scheduled.masterId)) || fallbackMaster;
  const currentParty =
    (scheduled && regionParties.find((p) => p.id === scheduled.partyId)) || fallbackParty;

  const currentMasterIdx = Math.max(0, regionMasters.findIndex((m) => m.id === currentMaster.id));
  const currentPartyIdx = Math.max(0, regionParties.findIndex((p) => p.id === currentParty.id));

  const nextMaster = regionMasters[(currentMasterIdx + 1) % Math.max(1, regionMasters.length)] || currentMaster;
  const nextParty = regionParties[(currentPartyIdx + 1) % Math.max(1, regionParties.length)] || currentParty;

  return { currentMaster, currentParty, nextMaster, nextParty };
}

export function synthesizeAlchemyProduct(
  reagents: [Reagent, Reagent, Reagent, Reagent, Reagent],
  customName: string,
  chelateSkillUnlocked: boolean
): CraftedProduct {
  const [base, active, carrier, stabiliser, catalyst] = reagents;
  const all = [base, active, carrier, stabiliser, catalyst];
  const componentIds = all.map((r) => r.id);
  const appliedSkills = chelateSkillUnlocked ? ['chelate_boost'] : [];

  const totalCost = Math.round(all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.baseCost, 0) * 0.85);
  const rawPotency = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.potency, 0);
  const rawStability = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.stability, 0) + (chelateSkillUnlocked ? 20 : 0);
  const rawVolatility = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.volatility, 0);
  const rawAcidity = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.acidity, 0);
  const rawToxicity = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.toxicity, 0);
  const rawAntiMagic = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.antiMagic, 0);
  const massEffectKg = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.massDeltaKg, 0);
  const netSulphurSens = all.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.traits.sulphurSensitivity, 0) - (chelateSkillUnlocked ? 30 : 0);

  const potency = Math.max(15, Math.min(100, Math.round(rawPotency * 0.62)));
  const stability = Math.max(10, Math.min(100, Math.round(rawStability * 0.65 - rawVolatility * 0.15)));
  const reliability = Math.max(20, Math.min(99, Math.round((potency + stability) / 2)));

  const tags = new Set(all.flatMap((r) => r.tags));
  const curesPetrification =
    tags.has('antipetrify') || active.id === 'reg_act_01' || (rawPotency >= 150 && rawAntiMagic >= 60);
  const causesPetrification = tags.has('petrify_hazard') || active.id === 'reg_act_07';
  const emitsSulphurFog = tags.has('fog_generator') || (tags.has('sulphur') && tags.has('aerosol'));
  const filtersSulphur =
    tags.has('sulphur_filter') ||
    (tags.has('sulphur_shield') && stability >= 70) ||
    (stability >= 82 && netSulphurSens <= 0);
  const isTrapSolvent = tags.has('trap_solvent') || rawAcidity >= 75;

  const sulphurVulnerable = netSulphurSens > 25 && !filtersSulphur;

  const counterClasses: CounterClass[] = [];
  if (massEffectKg <= -25) counterClasses.push('bypass_mass');
  if (curesPetrification || tags.has('healing') || rawToxicity < 25) counterClasses.push('resistance');
  if (filtersSulphur) counterClasses.push('filtration');
  if (isTrapSolvent) counterClasses.push('sabotage');
  if (rawAntiMagic >= 50 || tags.has('anti_magic')) counterClasses.push('disruption');
  if (tags.has('stealth')) counterClasses.push('detection');

  const isOffensive = causesPetrification || emitsSulphurFog || (rawToxicity >= 55 && !isTrapSolvent);
  const category = isTrapSolvent
    ? 'DUAL_SOLVENT'
    : filtersSulphur
    ? 'COUNTER_GEAR'
    : isOffensive
    ? 'HAZARD_MONSTER'
    : 'TONIC_HERO';

  const targetAudience = isOffensive ? 'MASTER' : isTrapSolvent ? 'BOTH' : 'HERO';

  const reactionNotes: string[] = [];
  if (curesPetrification) {
    reactionNotes.push(`Reverses Basilisk/Gorgon petrification (Potency ${potency}).`);
    if (sulphurVulnerable) {
      reactionNotes.push('SULPHUR VULNERABILITY: Loses 55% potency in Sulphur Fog! Add Calcified Quartz or Chelating Stabiliser.');
    } else {
      reactionNotes.push('SULPHUR IMMUNE: Chelating matrix protects antidote inside Sulphur Fog.');
    }
  }
  if (massEffectKg <= -25) {
    reactionNotes.push(`Reduces drinker mass by ${Math.abs(massEffectKg)}kg — bypasses pressure plates above hero’s shrunken weight.`);
  }
  if (filtersSulphur) {
    reactionNotes.push('Scrubs ambient Sulphur Fog and protects companion potions from chemical degradation.');
  }
  if (isTrapSolvent) {
    reactionNotes.push(`Corrosive Acidity (${rawAcidity}) dissolves non-anodised mechanical trap springs.`);
  }
  if (emitsSulphurFog) {
    reactionNotes.push('Generates choking Sulphur Fog that blinds scouts and weakens unstabilised hero potions.');
  }
  if (reactionNotes.length === 0) {
    reactionNotes.push(`Balanced alchemical brew (Potency ${potency}, Stability ${stability}).`);
  }

  const formulaSignature = computeCanonicalFormulaSignature('alc', componentIds, appliedSkills, SIM_RULES_VERSION);
  const canonicalId = computeCanonicalProductId('alc', componentIds, appliedSkills, SIM_RULES_VERSION);

  const defaultName =
    customName.trim() ||
    `${stabiliser.name.split(' ')[0]} ${active.name.split(' ').slice(-2).join(' ')} ${carrier.name.split(' ').pop()}`;

  return {
    id: canonicalId,
    formulaSignature,
    rulesVersion: SIM_RULES_VERSION,
    name: defaultName,
    category,
    targetAudience,
    productionCost: totalCost,
    suggestedPrice: Math.round(totalCost * 2.05),
    stock: 2,
    potency,
    stability,
    reliability,
    massEffectKg,
    sulphurVulnerable,
    emitsSulphurFog,
    causesPetrification,
    curesPetrification,
    filtersSulphur,
    triggerMassThresholdKg: 0,
    detectionDC: 0,
    damageType: emitsSulphurFog ? 'sulphur_fog' : causesPetrification ? 'petrification' : isTrapSolvent ? 'acid' : 'none',
    counterClasses: counterClasses.length > 0 ? counterClasses : ['resistance'],
    recipeComponents: componentIds,
    appliedSkills,
    description: `Synthesized from ${base.name}, ${active.name}, ${carrier.name}, ${stabiliser.name}, and ${catalyst.name} [Formula #${formulaSignature}].`,
    reactionNotes
  };
}

export function assembleTrapProduct(
  parts: [TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart],
  customName: string,
  zincGuardUnlocked: boolean
): CraftedProduct {
  const [trigger, concealment, delivery, payload, power, reset] = parts;
  const all = [trigger, concealment, delivery, payload, power, reset];
  const componentIds = all.map((p) => p.id);
  const appliedSkills = zincGuardUnlocked ? ['zinc_guard'] : [];

  const totalCost = Math.round(all.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.baseCost, 0) * 0.85);
  const rawLethality = all.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.lethality, 0);
  const rawDeterrence = all.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.deterrence, 0);
  const avgDetection = Math.round(
    trigger.detectionDC * 0.35 + concealment.detectionDC * 0.45 + delivery.detectionDC * 0.2
  );

  const potency = Math.max(20, Math.min(100, Math.round(rawLethality * 0.55)));
  const stability = Math.max(25, Math.min(100, Math.round(rawDeterrence * 0.52)));
  const reliability = Math.max(35, Math.min(99, Math.round((potency + stability + avgDetection) / 3)));

  const massThreshold = trigger.massThresholdKg ?? 0;
  const emitsSulphurFog =
    payload.damageType === 'sulphur_fog' ||
    delivery.damageType === 'sulphur_fog' ||
    concealment.damageType === 'sulphur_fog';
  const causesPetrification =
    payload.damageType === 'petrification' || delivery.damageType === 'petrification';

  const counterSet = new Set<CounterClass>(trigger.counterplayRoutes);
  payload.counterplayRoutes.forEach((c) => counterSet.add(c));
  if (zincGuardUnlocked || reset.id === 'part_rst_06') {
    counterSet.delete('sabotage');
  }

  const reactionNotes: string[] = [
    `Trigger Mass Threshold: ${massThreshold > 0 ? `${massThreshold}kg` : '0kg (Non-Mass Sensor)'} · Detection DC ${avgDetection}`
  ];
  if (trigger.id === 'part_trg_12') {
    reactionNotes.push('MASS INVERSION: triggers below 25kg; heavier intruders bypass the sensor.');
  } else if (massThreshold >= 35) {
    reactionNotes.push(`MASS BYPASS WINDOW: Heroes shrunk below ${massThreshold}kg via Chicken-Morph Draught will not trigger this plate!`);
  } else if (massThreshold > 0 && massThreshold <= 12) {
    reactionNotes.push(`ANTI-MORPH HAIR TRIGGER: ${massThreshold}kg threshold catches even Chicken-Morphed heroes!`);
  }
  if (reset.id === 'part_rst_09') reactionNotes.push('FINITE TEMPORAL RESET: one charge per paid installation prevents one wear removal; no free replenishment.');
  if (emitsSulphurFog) {
    reactionNotes.push('SULPHUR ATMOSPHERE: Degrades unstabilised hero antidotes by 55% unless filtered.');
  }
  if (causesPetrification) {
    reactionNotes.push('PETRIFICATION HAZARD: Calcifies heroes lacking a functional Basilisk Antidote.');
  }
  if (zincGuardUnlocked || reset.id === 'part_rst_06') {
    reactionNotes.push('ANODISED ZINC SHIELD: Immune to Rust-Monster Solvent sabotage.');
  }

  const formulaSignature = computeCanonicalFormulaSignature('trp', componentIds, appliedSkills, SIM_RULES_VERSION);
  const canonicalId = computeCanonicalProductId('trp', componentIds, appliedSkills, SIM_RULES_VERSION);

  const defaultName =
    customName.trim() ||
    `${trigger.name.split(' ')[0]} ${payload.name.split(' ').slice(0, 2).join(' ')} ${delivery.name.split(' ').pop()}`;

  return {
    id: canonicalId,
    formulaSignature,
    rulesVersion: SIM_RULES_VERSION,
    name: defaultName,
    category: causesPetrification ? 'HAZARD_MONSTER' : 'TRAP_MASTER',
    targetAudience: 'MASTER',
    productionCost: totalCost,
    suggestedPrice: Math.round(totalCost * 2.05),
    stock: 2,
    potency,
    stability,
    reliability,
    massEffectKg: 0,
    sulphurVulnerable: false,
    emitsSulphurFog,
    causesPetrification,
    curesPetrification: false,
    filtersSulphur: false,
    triggerMassThresholdKg: massThreshold,
    detectionDC: avgDetection,
    damageType: payload.damageType,
    counterClasses: Array.from(counterSet),
    recipeComponents: componentIds,
    appliedSkills,
    description: `Engineered with ${trigger.name}, ${concealment.name}, ${delivery.name}, and ${payload.name} [Build #${formulaSignature}].`,
    reactionNotes
  };
}

export function simulateRegionExpedition(
  day: number,
  seed: number,
  master: DungeonMaster,
  party: AdventuringParty,
  productsCatalog: CraftedProduct[],
  contractsToday: SaleContract[]
): ExpeditionReport {
  const rng = new DeterministicRNG(seed + day * 7919);

  // Gather active hazards for master and active gear for party strictly by canonical ID
  const masterProducts = master.installedHazards
    .map((id) => productsCatalog.find((p) => p.id === id))
    .filter((p): p is CraftedProduct => Boolean(p));

  // If master has 0 installed player traps, dungeon operates with inherent environmental hazards (Gate 3)
  // We NEVER inject fake consumable player sales (e.g. prod_trap_plate_spikes) into player catalog or master!
  const effectiveMasterHazards: CraftedProduct[] =
    masterProducts.length > 0
      ? masterProducts
      : [
          {
            id: 'env_baseline_masonry',
            name: 'Ancient Subterranean Flagstone Chokepoint',
            category: 'TRAP_MASTER',
            targetAudience: 'MASTER',
            stock: 1,
            productionCost: 0,
            suggestedPrice: 0,
            stability: 100,
            potency: 22,
            reliability: 100,
            massEffectKg: 0,
            sulphurVulnerable: false,
            emitsSulphurFog: false,
            causesPetrification: false,
            curesPetrification: false,
            filtersSulphur: false,
            triggerMassThresholdKg: 65,
            detectionDC: 30,
            damageType: 'physical',
            recipeComponents: [],
            counterClasses: [],
            description:
              'Permanent inherent dungeon architectural masonry and natural collapsing stone slabs.',
            reactionNotes: ['Inherent dungeon architectural masonry']
          }
        ];

  const partyProducts = party.equippedProducts
    .map((id) => productsCatalog.find((p) => p.id === id))
    .filter((p): p is CraftedProduct => Boolean(p));

  // Check if Sulphur Fog is present anywhere in Master's setup
  const dungeonHasSulphurFog = effectiveMasterHazards.some((p) => p.emitsSulphurFog);
  const partyHasSulphurFilter = partyProducts.some((p) => p.filtersSulphur);
  const morphPotion = partyProducts.find((p) => p.massEffectKg <= -25) || null;
  const rustSolvent =
    partyProducts.find((p) => p.counterClasses.includes('sabotage') && p.category === 'DUAL_SOLVENT') || null;
  const basiliskAntidote = partyProducts.find((p) => p.curesPetrification) || null;

  const roomNames = [
    'Threshold Flagstone Gallery',
    'Smelting Ventilation Sump',
    'Inner Treasury Vault'
  ];

  const rooms: ExpeditionRoomEvent[] = [];
  let totalLooted = 0;
  let warrantyClaimsTriggered = 0;
  const doubleSaleDemandCreated: string[] = [];

  const vanguard = party.members[0] || {
    name: 'Lead Delver',
    massKg: 85,
    hp: 100,
    maxHp: 100,
    role: 'Vanguard',
    status: 'Healthy'
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const hazard = effectiveMasterHazards[i % effectiveMasterHazards.length];
    const baseMass = vanguard.massKg;
    const effectiveMass = Math.max(12, baseMass + (morphPotion ? morphPotion.massEffectKg : 0));
    const threshold = hazard.triggerMassThresholdKg;

    let outcome: ExpeditionRoomEvent['outcome'] = 'TRAP_TRIGGERED';
    let heroCounterUsed: string | null = null;
    let chemicalReaction: string | null = null;
    let damageDealt = 0;
    let lootSecured = 0;
    let casualtyName: string | null = null;
    let causalExplanation = '';
    let learnedByHeroes = '';
    let learnedByMaster = '';

    // 1. Check Mass Bypass / Mass-Inversion on Pressure Plate triggers
    const inversion = isMassInversionPlate(hazard);
    const triggersByMass = isTrapTriggeredByMass(hazard, effectiveMass);
    const hasTemporalRewind =
      hazard.recipeComponents.includes('part_rst_09');

    if (inversion && triggersByMass) {
      // Inversion Plate triggers on abnormally light (<25kg) featherweight morphs!
      outcome = 'TRAP_TRIGGERED';
      damageDealt = Math.round(hazard.potency * 0.65) + rng.nextInt(12, 28);
      lootSecured = rng.nextInt(30, 80);
      chemicalReaction = `PARADOX MASS-INVERSION: Inversion sensor detected abnormally light ${effectiveMass}kg intruder (<25kg)! Gravitational polarity inverted and plate detonated!`;
      if (hasTemporalRewind) {
        chemicalReaction += ` [Temporal Rewind Loop: Finite reset module: remaining paid charge is settled against expedition wear.]`;
      }
      causalExplanation = `${party.name} attempted a featherweight ${effectiveMass}kg morph bypass, but ${hazard.name} inverted gravitational polarity and detonated on the lightweight intruder!`;
      learnedByHeroes = `Discovered ${hazard.name} inverts mass physics: featherweight morphs trigger the trap! Demanding anti-arcane disruption.`;
      learnedByMaster = `${master.name}'s Paradox Mass-Inversion Plate successfully caught the featherweight intruders.`;
      doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${party.name} seeks anti-arcane countermeasures against Mass Inversion`);
    } else if (!triggersByMass) {
      outcome = 'TRAP_BYPASSED';
      heroCounterUsed = morphPotion?.name ?? 'Naturally light vanguard';
      chemicalReaction = `Mass Rule: ${vanguard.name} (${baseMass}kg) drank ${morphPotion?.name ?? 'natural light mass'} (${morphPotion?.massEffectKg ?? 0}kg) -> ${effectiveMass}kg < ${threshold}kg Plate Threshold!`;
      if (hasTemporalRewind) {
        chemicalReaction += ` [Temporal Rewind Loop: Finite reset module: bypass alone does not consume a reset charge.]`;
      }
      damageDealt = 0;
      lootSecured = rng.nextInt(180, 310);
      causalExplanation = `${party.name} bypassed ${hazard.name} completely because their shrunken ${effectiveMass}kg mass failed to depress the ${threshold}kg pressure plate.`;
      if (inversion) {
        chemicalReaction = `Mass Rule: ${effectiveMass}kg is outside the inversion sensor's under-25kg window.`;
        causalExplanation = `${party.name} bypassed ${hazard.name}: inversion sensors trigger only below 25kg.`;
      }
      learnedByHeroes = `Confirmed ${morphPotion?.name ?? 'natural light mass'} bypasses ${threshold}kg plates in ${master.name}'s dungeon.`;
      learnedByMaster = `${master.name} furious that ${threshold}kg plates were bypassed by lightweight heroes; now demands <10kg Hair-Trigger Quartz traps or Mass-Inversion Plates!`;
      doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} demands Mass-Inversion Plates to counter ${morphPotion?.name ?? 'natural light mass'}`);
    }
    // 2. Check Basilisk Petrification + Sulphur Fog Chemical Reaction (The Basilisk Supply Chain!)
    else if (hazard.causesPetrification) {
      if (basiliskAntidote) {
        if (dungeonHasSulphurFog && basiliskAntidote.sulphurVulnerable && !partyHasSulphurFilter) {
          outcome = 'REACTION_FAILURE';
          heroCounterUsed = basiliskAntidote.name;
          chemicalReaction = `ALCHEMICAL INTERFERENCE: Ambient Sulphur Fog reacted with unstabilised ${basiliskAntidote.name} (-55% potency)!`;
          damageDealt = rng.nextInt(42, 68);
          lootSecured = rng.nextInt(40, 90);
          casualtyName = `${vanguard.name} (Partial Petrification)`;
          causalExplanation = `Sulphur Fog obscured the Basilisk and chemically degraded ${party.name}'s unstabilised ${basiliskAntidote.name}. Two heroes retreated with stone-calcified limbs!`;
          learnedByHeroes = `Discovered ${basiliskAntidote.name} fails in Sulphur Fog! Demanding Quartz-Chelated Antidotes or Charcoal Breathers.`;
          learnedByMaster = `${master.name} saw Sulphur Fog + Basilisk repel the raid, but worries zero survivors means fewer future ticketed raids.`;
          doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${party.name} urgently demands Quartz-Chelated Antidotes & Charcoal Breathers`);
          warrantyClaimsTriggered += 1;
        } else {
          outcome = 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED';
          heroCounterUsed = partyHasSulphurFilter
            ? `${basiliskAntidote.name} + Charcoal Breather`
            : basiliskAntidote.name;
          chemicalReaction = dungeonHasSulphurFog
            ? `Chelated/Filtered Antidote resisted Sulphur Fog and neutralised petrification gaze!`
            : `Active ${basiliskAntidote.name} reversed stone calcification on contact.`;
          damageDealt = rng.nextInt(8, 18);
          lootSecured = rng.nextInt(240, 390);
          causalExplanation = `${party.name} neutralised ${hazard.name} using stabilised anti-petrification chemistry and plundered the chamber.`;
          learnedByHeroes = `Verified ${basiliskAntidote.name} holds up under combat conditions.`;
          learnedByMaster = `${master.name}'s Basilisk was countered! Demands higher-lethality Acid or Arcane traps next turn.`;
          doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} seeks Acid/Arcane upgrades after Basilisk was countered`);
        }
      } else {
        outcome = 'TRAP_TRIGGERED';
        damageDealt = rng.nextInt(55, 85);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(20, 60);
        casualtyName = `${vanguard.name} (Petrified)`;
        causalExplanation = `${party.name} entered without a Basilisk Antidote and suffered severe petrification from ${hazard.name}.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `Refuses to re-enter without Basilisk Antidotes!`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name}'s Basilisk scored a decisive deterrent victory.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${party.name} desperate to buy Basilisk Antidotes`);
      }
    }
    // 3. Check Rust-Monster Solvent Sabotage
    else if (rustSolvent && hazard.counterClasses.includes('sabotage')) {
      outcome = 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED';
      heroCounterUsed = rustSolvent.name;
      chemicalReaction = `Corrosive Oxidation: ${rustSolvent.name} dissolved ${hazard.name}'s unplated iron springs!`;
      damageDealt = 5;
      lootSecured = rng.nextInt(210, 340);
      causalExplanation = `${party.name}'s scout shattered a ${rustSolvent.name} on the mechanism, seizing the gears before actuation.`;
      learnedByHeroes = `Rust solvent reliably disables unplated clockwork traps.`;
      learnedByMaster = `${master.name} demands Anodised Zinc Coating on future traps to stop acid sabotage.`;
      doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} demands Zinc-Anodised traps immune to Rust Solvent`);
    }
    // 4. Check Sulphur Fog vs Charcoal Breather
    else if (hazard.emitsSulphurFog) {
      if (partyHasSulphurFilter) {
        const breather = partyProducts.find((p) => p.filtersSulphur)!;
        outcome = 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED';
        heroCounterUsed = breather.name;
        chemicalReaction = `Adsorption Filtration: Alkali Charcoal scrubbed 94% of sulphuric vapour.`;
        damageDealt = rng.nextInt(6, 14);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(190, 300);
        causalExplanation = `${party.name} breathed cleanly through ${hazard.name} using ${breather.name}.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `Charcoal filtration completely negates sulphur vents.`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name} notes heroes now carry breathers; wants kinetic Scythes or 8kg Acid Sprayers instead.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} pivots from Sulphur Fog to Kinetic/Acid traps`);
      } else {
        outcome = 'TRAP_TRIGGERED';
        damageDealt = rng.nextInt(32, 54);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(75, 140);
        causalExplanation = `Dense yellow clouds from ${hazard.name} choked ${party.name} and blinded their scout.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `Needs Charcoal Alchemical Breathers before next delve.`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name} pleased with Sulphur Fog area denial.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${party.name} demands Charcoal Alchemical Breathers`);
      }
    }
    // 5. Standard Trap Resolution
    else {
      const heroHasResistance = partyProducts.some((p) => p.counterClasses.includes('resistance') && p.potency >= 60);
      const scoutRoll = rng.nextInt(25, 75) + Math.round(party.caution * 0.25);
      if (scoutRoll >= hazard.detectionDC) {
        outcome = 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED';
        heroCounterUsed = 'Scout Perception & Careful Routing';
        damageDealt = rng.nextInt(5, 15);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(170, 280);
        causalExplanation = `${party.name} spotted the ${hazard.name} (DC ${hazard.detectionDC} vs Roll ${scoutRoll}) and wedged the release latch.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `Mapped the trigger layout of ${hazard.name}.`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name} wants higher Concealment DC modules so scouts stop spotting traps.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} demands higher Concealment DC upgrades`);
      } else if (heroHasResistance && hazard.potency <= 78) {
        const resistPotion = partyProducts.find((p) => p.counterClasses.includes('resistance'))!;
        outcome = 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED';
        heroCounterUsed = resistPotion.name;
        chemicalReaction = `Protective Ward: ${resistPotion.name} (Potency ${resistPotion.potency}) absorbed the brunt of ${hazard.name}.`;
        damageDealt = rng.nextInt(10, 22);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(155, 255);
        causalExplanation = `${hazard.name} triggered, but ${party.name}'s ${resistPotion.name} mitigated the ${hazard.damageType} payload.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `${resistPotion.name} cushioned ${hazard.name}'s impact.`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name} wants higher-potency payloads to overwhelm hero tonics.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${master.name} seeks higher-lethality payloads`);
      } else {
        outcome = 'TRAP_TRIGGERED';
        damageDealt = Math.round(hazard.potency * 0.55) + rng.nextInt(8, 22);
        lootSecured = rng.nextInt(60, 130);
        if (damageDealt >= 52) {
          casualtyName = `${party.members[party.members.length - 1]?.name || 'Scout'} (Injured Retreat)`;
        }
        causalExplanation = `${hazard.name} caught ${party.name} off-guard (DC ${hazard.detectionDC}), dealing ${damageDealt} ${hazard.damageType} damage.`;
        learnedByHeroes = `Recorded ${hazard.name} in guild hazard registry; seeking specific counter-gear.`;
        learnedByMaster = `${master.name} satisfied with ${hazard.name}'s performance.`;
        doubleSaleDemandCreated.push(`${party.name} seeks counter-tonics against ${hazard.damageType} hazards`);
      }
    }

    totalLooted += lootSecured;
    rooms.push({
      roomIndex: i + 1,
      roomName: roomNames[i],
      hazardName: hazard.name,
      hazardId: hazard.id,
      triggerType: threshold > 0 ? `${threshold}kg Pressure Trigger` : 'Sensor/Trip Trigger',
      triggerMassThresholdKg: threshold,
      heroEffectiveMassKg: effectiveMass,
      heroCounterUsed,
      chemicalReaction,
      outcome,
      damageDealt,
      lootSecured,
      casualtyName,
      causalExplanation,
      learnedByHeroes,
      learnedByMaster
    });
  }

  // Check if player breached an exclusivity contract today (sold to both Master and Hero in same region when one had Exclusive Deal)
  // Wax-Removed Maker Marks (secretProvenance) reduces double-dealing discovery chance by 50%
  const masterSalesToday = contractsToday.filter((c) => c.buyerId === master.id);
  const heroSalesToday = contractsToday.filter((c) => c.buyerId === party.id);
  const hasExclusiveDoubleSale =
    masterSalesToday.length > 0 &&
    heroSalesToday.length > 0 &&
    [...masterSalesToday, ...heroSalesToday].some((c) => c.exclusiveDeal);
  const allMaskedWithNullWax =
    hasExclusiveDoubleSale &&
    [...masterSalesToday, ...heroSalesToday]
      .filter((c) => c.exclusiveDeal)
      .every((c) => c.secretProvenance);
  const exclusivityBreachDetected =
    hasExclusiveDoubleSale && (!allMaskedWithNullWax || rng.next() < 0.5);

  const totalDamage = rooms.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.damageDealt, 0);
  const masterTreasuryDelta = Math.round(totalDamage * 4.5 - totalLooted * 0.45 + 180);
  const partyBudgetDelta = Math.round(totalLooted - totalDamage * 2.2);

  const reactionFailureRoom = rooms.find((r) => r.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE');
  const bypassRoom = rooms.find((r) => r.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED');

  const summaryHeadline = reactionFailureRoom
    ? `Sulphur Fog Degraded ${party.name}'s Antidotes in ${master.name}'s Lair!`
    : bypassRoom
    ? `${party.name} Used Featherweight Morph to Walk Over ${master.name}'s Pressure Plates!`
    : `${party.name} Raided ${master.name}: ${totalLooted}g Looted, ${totalDamage} Hazard Damage Dealt`;

  return {
    id: `exp_day${day}_${master.id}_${party.id}`,
    day,
    seed,
    rulesVersion: SIM_RULES_VERSION,
    region: master.region,
    masterId: master.id,
    masterName: master.name,
    partyId: party.id,
    partyName: party.name,
    masterSupplies: masterProducts.map((p) => p.name),
    heroSupplies: partyProducts.map((p) => p.name),
    masterSupplyIds: masterProducts.map((p) => p.id),
    heroSupplyIds: partyProducts.map((p) => p.id),
    rooms,
    totalLooted,
    masterTreasuryDelta,
    partyBudgetDelta,
    warrantyClaimsTriggered,
    exclusivityBreachDetected,
    doubleSaleDemandCreated: Array.from(new Set(doubleSaleDemandCreated)),
    summaryHeadline
  };
}

export function generatePeerCatalystCode(senderName: string, day: number): string {
  const clean = senderName.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase().slice(0, 8) || 'MERCHANT';
  const sig = hashString(`PARADOX_CATALYST_${clean}_${day}`).slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  return `CATALYST-${clean}-D${day}-${sig}`;
}

/**
 * F03 Fix: Enforces Hard-mode access ('blackspire' unlocked), cryptographic provenance hash verification,
 * and local replay protection before allowing a Peer Catalyst code to unlock The Paradox Vault.
 */
export function verifyAndParseCatalystCode(
  code: string,
  currentDay: number,
  existingLedger: ParadoxCatalystEntry[],
  unlockedRegions: RegionId[] = ['cellar', 'warrens', 'blackspire']
): { valid: boolean; error?: string; entry?: ParadoxCatalystEntry } {
  if (!unlockedRegions.includes('blackspire')) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'Hard Mode Prerequisite Not Met: You must unlock Blackspire Labyrinth (Region III) before a Paradox Catalyst can attune to The Paradox Vault.'
    };
  }

  const trimmed = code.trim().toUpperCase();
  const match = /^CATALYST-([A-Z0-9]{3,12})-D(\d+)-([A-Z0-9]{6,12})$/.exec(trimmed);
  if (!match) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'Invalid Catalyst Manifest format. Expected CATALYST-<SENDER>-D<DAY>-<HASH>.'
    };
  }

  const [, sender, dayStr, hashPart] = match;
  const expectedSig = hashString(`PARADOX_CATALYST_${sender}_${dayStr}`).slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();

  if (hashPart !== expectedSig) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: `Format Checksum Mismatch: Hash #${hashPart} does not match the deterministic algorithm for sender ${sender} (Day ${dayStr}).`
    };
  }

  if (existingLedger.some((e) => e.provenanceHash.toUpperCase() === hashPart)) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      error: 'Local Replay Detection: This Catalyst provenance hash has already been recorded in your Gift Ledger.'
    };
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    entry: {
      id: `cat_peer_d${currentDay}_${hashPart.toLowerCase()}`,
      senderName: `Community Envoy ${sender}`,
      senderType: 'PEER_QR_EXCHANGE',
      provenanceHash: hashPart,
      dayReceived: currentDay,
      commissionNote: `Community merchant exchange code verified via format checksum (${trimmed}). Unauthenticated casual transfer.`,
      isAuthentic: false
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Pre-expedition deterministic forecast (Gate 4).
 * Previews room encounters and outcomes matching actual simulateRegionExpedition resolution.
 */
export function predictExpeditionForecast(
  day: number,
  seed: number,
  master: DungeonMaster,
  party: AdventuringParty,
  productsCatalog: CraftedProduct[],
  contractsToday: SaleContract[] = []
): {
  predictedRooms: ExpeditionRoomEvent[];
  summaryHeadline: string;
  expectedLoot: number;
} {
  const sim = simulateRegionExpedition(day, seed, master, party, productsCatalog, contractsToday);
  return {
    predictedRooms: sim.rooms,
    summaryHeadline: sim.summaryHeadline,
    expectedLoot: sim.totalLooted
  };
}
