                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-slate-200">
                    PRE-EXPEDITION INTELLIGENCE &amp; CAUSAL FORECAST
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400">
                  {expeditionForecast.summaryHeadline}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                {expeditionForecast.predictedRooms.map((rm) => (
                  <div
                    key={`forecast_${rm.roomIndex}`}
                    className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800/80 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono space-y-1.5"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
                      <span>CHAMBER 0{rm.roomIndex}</span>
                      <span
                        className={
                          rm.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED' || rm.outcome === 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED'
                            ? 'text-emerald-400 font-semibold'
                            : rm.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE'
                            ? 'text-amber-400 font-semibold'
                            : 'text-rose-400 font-semibold'
                        }
                      >
                        {rm.outcome}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-slate-200 font-semibold truncate">{rm.hazardName}</div>
                    <div className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
                      {rm.causalExplanation}
                    </div>
                    {rm.chemicalReaction?.includes('PARADOX MASS-INVERSION') && (
                      <div className="text-amber-400 text-[11px] font-semibold bg-amber-950/40 p-1.5 rounded border border-amber-800/50">
                        ⚠ Inversion Detonation: Lightweight morph triggers inverted polarity!
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {rm.chemicalReaction?.includes('Temporal Rewind Loop') && (
                      <div className="text-cyan-400 text-[11px] bg-cyan-950/40 p-1.5 rounded border border-cyan-800/50">
                        ↻ Temporal module: one paid charge prevents one wear removal; settlement persists after the raid.
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* 60fps Live Dungeon Expedition Viewport with >=85% Unobstructed Playfield & pointer-events:none HUD */}
            <div className="relative w-full h-[380px] sm:h-[430px] rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-800 bg-[#0B0E14]">
              <ExpeditionCanvas
                mode="EXPEDITION_LIVE"
                report={activeCanvasReport}
                activeRoomIdx={activeRoomIdx}
                simSpeed={a11y.simSpeed}
                reducedMotion={a11y.reducedMotion}
                onRoomAdvance={setActiveRoomIdx}
              />

            </div>
            {/* Status and room controls occupy their own flow; never cover the touch playfield. */}
            <div className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="bg-[#0B0E14]/90 border border-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-slate-200">
                    <span>
                      RAID: {activeCanvasReport.partyName} vs {activeCanvasReport.masterName}
                    </span>
                    <span className="mx-2">·</span>
                    <span className="text-amber-400">SEED #{activeCanvasReport.seed}</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="bg-[#0B0E14]/90 border border-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-emerald-400">
                    LOOTED: +{activeCanvasReport.totalLooted}g · MASTER Δ:{' '}
                    {activeCanvasReport.masterTreasuryDelta >= 0
                      ? `+${activeCanvasReport.masterTreasuryDelta}g`
                      : `${activeCanvasReport.masterTreasuryDelta}g`}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="bg-[#0B0E14]/90 border border-slate-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
                    Drag to pan · WASD / arrows · Space steps rooms ({activeRoomIdx + 1}/{activeCanvasReport.rooms.length})
                  </div>
                  <div className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-2">
                    {activeCanvasReport.rooms.map((rm, idx) => (
                      <button
                        key={rm.roomIndex}
                        onClick={() => setActiveRoomIdx(idx)}
                        className={`min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                          activeRoomIdx === idx
                            ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                            : 'bg-[#0B0E14] text-slate-300 border border-slate-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Room {rm.roomIndex}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Causal Chain After-Action Breakdown */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              {activeCanvasReport.rooms.map((rm, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={rm.roomIndex}
                  onClick={() => setActiveRoomIdx(idx)}
                  className={`p-4 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                    activeRoomIdx === idx
                      ? 'bg-[#131822] border-amber-500/80'
                      : 'bg-[#131822] border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
                    <span>
                      ROOM 0{rm.roomIndex} · {rm.roomName}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={
                        rm.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED' || rm.outcome === 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED'
                          ? 'text-emerald-400 font-semibold'
                          : rm.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE'
                          ? 'text-amber-400 font-semibold'
                          : 'text-rose-400 font-semibold'
                      }
                    >
                      {rm.outcome}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100 mb-1">{rm.hazardName}</div>
                  {rm.chemicalReaction && (
                    <div className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-300 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 p-2 rounded mb-2">
                      {rm.chemicalReaction}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {rm.chemicalReaction?.includes('Temporal Rewind Loop') && (
                    <div className="text-xs font-mono text-cyan-300 bg-cyan-950/40 border border-cyan-800/60 p-2 rounded mb-2">
                      <div className="font-semibold mb-1">↻ Temporal Rewind Chronology:</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-cyan-200/90 space-y-0.5">
                        <div>· t=0.00s: Intruder steps onto pressure trigger</div>
                        <div>· t=0.18s: Detonation sequence actuates</div>
                        <div>· t=0.35s: Closed timelike loop re-cocks trigger mechanism</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-2">{rm.causalExplanation}</p>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 space-y-1 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                    <div>Hero Adaptation: {rm.learnedByHeroes}</div>
                    <div>Master Adaptation: {rm.learnedByMaster}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'DAY_DESK' && (
          <TradeFloorPanel
            day={gameState.day}
            seed={gameState.seed}
            activeRegion={gameState.activeRegion}
            products={gameState.products}
            masters={gameState.masters}
            parties={gameState.parties}
            scheduledMasterId={activeRegionSchedule.currentMaster.id}
            scheduledPartyId={activeRegionSchedule.currentParty.id}
            nextMasterName={activeRegionSchedule.nextMaster.name}
            nextPartyName={activeRegionSchedule.nextParty.name}
            contractsToday={gameState.contractsToday}
            bilateralSkillUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('bilateral_margin')}
            secretShippingUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('secret_shipping')}
            unlockedSkills={gameState.skills.filter((s) => s.unlocked).map((s) => s.effectKey)}
            tradeTerms={gameState.tradeTerms}
            onUpdateTradeTerms={(terms) =>
              setGameState((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                tradeTerms: terms
              }))
            }
            onSelectScheduledMatchup={handleSelectScheduledMatchup}
            onExecuteSale={handleExecuteSale}
            onLaunchExpedition={handleLaunchExpedition}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'ALCHEMY_LAB' && (
          <AlchemyLabPanel
            gold={gameState.gold}
            unlockedRegions={gameState.unlockedRegions}
            reagentInventory={gameState.reagentInventory}
            chelateSkillUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('chelate_boost')}
            bonusYieldUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('bonus_brew_yield')}
            onBrewProduct={handleBrewProduct}
            onBuyReagent={handleBuyReagent}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'TRAP_WORKSHOP' && (
          <TrapWorkshopPanel
            gold={gameState.gold}
            unlockedRegions={gameState.unlockedRegions}
            partInventory={gameState.partInventory}
            zincGuardUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('zinc_guard')}
            onAssembleTrap={handleAssembleTrap}
            onBuyPart={handleBuyPart}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'INTEL_RUMOURS' && (
          <IntelAndStoryPanel
            day={gameState.day}
            gold={gameState.gold}
            intelFeed={gameState.intelFeed}
            basiliskChainStage={gameState.basiliskChainStage}
            blackspireUnlocked={gameState.unlockedRegions.includes('blackspire')}
            blackspireResolution={gameState.blackspireResolution}
            onResolveBasiliskDecision={handleResolveBasiliskDecision}
            onChooseBlackspireEnding={(ending) => {
              setGameState((prev) => ({ ...prev, blackspireResolution: ending }));
              setStatusBanner(`House Political Doctrine Set: ${ending.replace('_', ' ')}.`);
            }}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'FINANCIALS' && (
          <FinancialsPanel
            gold={gameState.gold}
            debt={gameState.debt}
            unlockedRegions={gameState.unlockedRegions}
            expertUnlocked={gameState.expertUnlocked}
            ledger={gameState.financialLedger}
            rivals={gameState.rivals}
            cheapCreditUnlocked={isSkillUnlocked('cheap_credit')}
            onUnlockRegionCharter={handleUnlockRegionCharter}
            onPayDownDebt={(amt) => {
              setGameState((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                gold: prev.gold - amt,
                debt: Math.max(0, prev.debt - amt)
              }));
              setStatusBanner(`Repaid ${amt}g of Dwarven Bridge Financing.`);
            }}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'CLIENT_LEDGER' && (
          <ClientDirectoryPanel masters={gameState.masters} parties={gameState.parties} />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'SPECIALISATIONS' && (
          <SpecialisationsPanel
            skillPoints={gameState.skillPoints}
            skills={gameState.skills}
            onUnlockSkill={(skillId) => {
              soundFX.playCoin();
              setGameState((prev) => {
                const target = prev.skills.find((s) => s.id === skillId);
                if (!target || target.unlocked || prev.skillPoints < target.costPoints) return prev;
                return {
                  ...prev,
                  skillPoints: prev.skillPoints - target.costPoints,
                  skills: prev.skills.map((s) => (s.id === skillId ? { ...s, unlocked: true } : s))
                };
              });
            }}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'ARCHIVES_CATALYST' && (
          <ArchivesAndCatalystPanel
            day={gameState.day}
            seed={gameState.seed}
            blackspireUnlocked={gameState.unlockedRegions.includes('blackspire')}
            expertUnlocked={gameState.expertUnlocked}
            catalystLedger={gameState.catalystLedger}
            expeditionReports={gameState.expeditionReports}
            customBrewsCount={gameState.customBrewsCount}
            customTrapsCount={gameState.customTrapsCount}
            masterSalesCount={masterSalesCount}
            heroSalesCount={heroSalesCount}
            onClaimSoloDmCatalyst={handleClaimSoloDmCatalyst}
            onImportPeerCatalyst={handleImportPeerCatalyst}
            onExportSaveJson={() => JSON.stringify(gameState)}
            onImportSaveJson={(jsonStr) => {
              const check = validateSavePayload(jsonStr);
              if (!check.valid || !check.state) {
                try { quarantinePayload(localStorage, jsonStr); } catch { /* preserve checkpoints even when quota is full */ }
                setRecoveryNotice(`Import refused: ${check.error}. Current campaign unchanged.`);
                return false;
              }
              if (!persistAtomicSaveSafe(check.state, localStorage)) return false;
              saveBlocked.current = false;
              setGameState(check.state);
              setRecoveryNotice(`Imported validated ledger: Day ${check.state.day}.`);
              return true;
            }}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      {/* F06/F07 Fix: Ergonomic Bottom Thumb Navigation Bar for Mobile (<1024px) & One-Thumb Portrait Mode (Includes ALL 9 Tabs including Skills!) */}
      <nav
        aria-label="Mobile and One-Thumb Workspace Navigation"
        className={`${
          a11y.oneThumbPortraitMode ? 'flex' : 'flex lg:hidden'
        } fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 bg-[#0B0E14]/95 border-t border-slate-800 px-2 py-1.5 overflow-x-auto items-center justify-start sm:justify-center gap-1`}
      >
        {WORKSPACE_NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.id}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab(item.id)}
            className={`min-h-[44px] min-w-[64px] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono rounded-lg transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer flex flex-col items-center justify-center ${
              activeTab === item.id
                ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                : 'bg-[#131822] text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{item.shortLabel}</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </nav>

      {/* Pause & Accessibility Modal */}
      {shellState === 'PAUSED' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-700 rounded-xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 space-y-5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-100">
                Ledger Paused &amp; Accessibility Settings
              </h2>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShellState('PLAYING')}
                className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
              >
                Resume (Esc)
              </button>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              Accessibility controls are completely separate from gameplay difficulty and never restrict Paradox Catalyst or Expert Vault eligibility.
            </p>

            <div className="space-y-3 text-xs font-mono">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg">
                <span>Typography Scale</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  {(['normal', 'large', 'xlarge'] as const).map((sz) => (
                    <button
                      key={sz}
                      onClick={() => setA11y((prev) => ({ ...prev, fontScale: sz }))}
                      className={`min-h-[36px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded cursor-pointer ${
                        a11y.fontScale === sz ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sz.toUpperCase()}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg">
                <span>Expedition Simulation Speed</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  {([1, 2, 4] as const).map((spd) => (
                    <button
                      key={spd}
                      onClick={() => setA11y((prev) => ({ ...prev, simSpeed: spd }))}
                      className={`min-h-[36px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded cursor-pointer ${
                        a11y.simSpeed === spd ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {spd}x
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
                <span>One-Thumb Portrait Layout &amp; Bottom Dock</span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={a11y.oneThumbPortraitMode}
                  onChange={(e) => setA11y((prev) => ({ ...prev, oneThumbPortraitMode: e.target.checked }))}
                  className="accent-amber-500"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
                <span>Reduced Animation &amp; Zero Camera Shake</span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={a11y.reducedMotion}
                  onChange={(e) => setA11y((prev) => ({ ...prev, reducedMotion: e.target.checked }))}
                  className="accent-amber-500"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg cursor-pointer">
                <span>Mute Procedural Web Audio Synth</span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={a11y.audioMuted}
                  onChange={(e) => setA11y((prev) => ({ ...prev, audioMuted: e.target.checked }))}
                  className="accent-amber-500"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShellState('TITLE_MENU')}
                className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-mono rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                Return to Title Screen
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShellState('PLAYING')}
                className="min-h-[42px] px-5 py-2 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-xs font-mono rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                Resume Campaign
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

==== END FILE src/App.tsx ====

==== BEGIN FILE src/components/CraftingPanels.tsx BLOB a3ee958efd8d0b5d666621603498954e82666d73 ====
import { isTrapTriggeredByMass } from '../engine/simulation';
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Reagent,
  TrapPart,
  CraftedProduct,
  RegionId,
  ReagentSlotRole,
  TrapSlotRole
} from '../types/game';
import { REAGENTS_CATALOG, TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG } from '../data/catalog';
import { synthesizeAlchemyProduct, assembleTrapProduct } from '../engine/simulation';

interface AlchemyLabProps {
  gold: number;
  unlockedRegions: RegionId[];
  reagentInventory: Record<string, number>;
  chelateSkillUnlocked: boolean;
  bonusYieldUnlocked: boolean;
  onBrewProduct: (product: CraftedProduct, cost: number, consumedIds: string[], batchSize: number) => void;
  onBuyReagent: (reagent: Reagent, qty: number) => void;
}

export const AlchemyLabPanel: React.FC<AlchemyLabProps> = ({
  gold,
  unlockedRegions,
  reagentInventory,
  chelateSkillUnlocked,
  bonusYieldUnlocked,
  onBrewProduct,
  onBuyReagent
}) => {
  const availableReagents = useMemo(
    () => REAGENTS_CATALOG.filter((r) => unlockedRegions.includes(r.regionOrigin)),
    [unlockedRegions]
  );

  const bases = availableReagents.filter((r) => r.role === 'base');
  const actives = availableReagents.filter((r) => r.role === 'active');
  const carriers = availableReagents.filter((r) => r.role === 'carrier');
  const stabilisers = availableReagents.filter((r) => r.role === 'stabiliser');
  const catalysts = availableReagents.filter((r) => r.role === 'catalyst');

  const [baseId, setBaseId] = useState(bases[1]?.id || bases[0].id);
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState(actives[0].id);
  const [carrierId, setCarrierId] = useState(carriers[1]?.id || carriers[0].id);
  const [stabiliserId, setStabiliserId] = useState(stabilisers[0].id);
  const [catalystId, setCatalystId] = useState(catalysts[0].id);
  const [customName, setCustomName] = useState('');
  const [marketFilter, setMarketFilter] = useState<ReagentSlotRole | 'all'>('all');

  const selectedTuple = useMemo((): [Reagent, Reagent, Reagent, Reagent, Reagent] => {
    const b = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === baseId) || bases[0];
    const a = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === activeId) || actives[0];
    const c = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === carrierId) || carriers[0];
    const s = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === stabiliserId) || stabilisers[0];
    const k = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === catalystId) || catalysts[0];
    return [b, a, c, s, k];
  }, [baseId, activeId, carrierId, stabiliserId, catalystId, bases, actives, carriers, stabilisers, catalysts]);

  const previewProduct = useMemo(
    () => synthesizeAlchemyProduct(selectedTuple, customName, chelateSkillUnlocked),
    [selectedTuple, customName, chelateSkillUnlocked]
  );

  const loadPreset = (preset: 'unstabilised_antidote' | 'chelated_antidote' | 'chicken_morph' | 'charcoal_breather' | 'rust_solvent') => {
    if (preset === 'unstabilised_antidote') {
      setBaseId('reg_base_02');
      setActiveId('reg_act_01');
      setCarrierId('reg_car_02');
      setStabiliserId('reg_stb_01');
      setCatalystId('reg_cat_01');
      setCustomName('Standard Basilisk Antidote (Unstabilised)');
    } else if (preset === 'chelated_antidote') {
      setBaseId('reg_base_02');
      setActiveId('reg_act_01');
      setCarrierId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_car_05' : 'reg_car_02');
      setStabiliserId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_stb_03' : 'reg_stb_02');
      setCatalystId('reg_cat_02');
      setCustomName('Quartz-Chelated Basilisk Antidote');
    } else if (preset === 'chicken_morph') {
      setBaseId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_base_06' : 'reg_base_02');
      setActiveId('reg_act_02');
      setCarrierId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_car_04' : 'reg_car_02');
      setStabiliserId('reg_stb_02');
      setCatalystId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_cat_04' : 'reg_cat_01');
      setCustomName('Bantam Chicken-Morph Draught');
    } else if (preset === 'charcoal_breather') {
      setBaseId('reg_base_02');
      setActiveId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_act_06' : 'reg_act_04');
      setCarrierId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_car_05' : 'reg_car_03');
      setStabiliserId('reg_stb_02');
      setCatalystId('reg_cat_01');
      setCustomName('Charcoal Alchemical Breather Tonic');
    } else if (preset === 'rust_solvent') {
      setBaseId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_base_03' : 'reg_base_04');
      setActiveId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_act_08' : 'reg_act_03');
      setCarrierId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_car_06' : 'reg_car_03');
      setStabiliserId('reg_stb_01');
      setCatalystId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'reg_cat_03' : 'reg_cat_01');
      setCustomName('Rust-Monster Gear Solvent Bomb');
    }
  };

  // Calculate missing reagent cost if not in stock
  const missingCost = selectedTuple.reduce((acc, r) => {
    const have = reagentInventory[r.id] || 0;
    return acc + (have > 0 ? 0 : r.baseCost);
  }, 0);

  const batchUnits = bonusYieldUnlocked ? 3 : 2;

  const handleSynthesize = () => {
    if (gold < missingCost) return;
    onBrewProduct(
      previewProduct,
      missingCost,
      selectedTuple.map((r) => r.id),
      batchUnits
    );
    setCustomName('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
      {/* Left 7 Cols: 5-Slot Alchemy Reaction Bench */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
                01. Five-Slot Alchemical Reaction Bench
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Combine Base + Active Reagent + Carrier + Stabiliser + Catalyst. Traits interact deterministically.
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400 tabular-nums">
              {availableReagents.length} / 48 Reagents Unlocked
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Formula Quick-Presets for Double-Sale Chains */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <p className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-2">Load Canonical Formula Template:</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => loadPreset('chelated_antidote')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Quartz-Chelated Antidote (Sulphur Immune)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadPreset('chicken_morph')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Bantam Chicken-Morph (-65kg Mass)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadPreset('charcoal_breather')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sky-300 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Charcoal Sulphur Breather
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadPreset('rust_solvent')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-rose-300 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Rust-Monster Solvent Bomb
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 5 Reagent Slot Selectors */}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[
              { label: 'Slot 1 · Base Solvent', list: bases, val: baseId, set: setBaseId },
              { label: 'Slot 2 · Active Reagent', list: actives, val: activeId, set: setActiveId },
              { label: 'Slot 3 · Delivery Carrier', list: carriers, val: carrierId, set: setCarrierId },
              { label: 'Slot 4 · Chemical Stabiliser', list: stabilisers, val: stabiliserId, set: setStabiliserId },
              { label: 'Slot 5 · Reaction Catalyst', list: catalysts, val: catalystId, set: setCatalystId }
            ].map((slot) => {
              const currentObj = REAGENTS_CATALOG.find((r) => r.id === slot.val) || slot.list[0];
              const inStock = reagentInventory[currentObj.id] || 0;
              return (
                <div key={slot.label} className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800/90 rounded-lg">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1.5">
                    <label className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">{slot.label}</label>
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
                      Stock: {inStock} · Direct Buy: {currentObj.baseCost}g · Grade: {currentObj.grade}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <select
                    value={slot.val}
                    onChange={(e) => slot.set(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                  >
                    {slot.list.map((r) => (
                      <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                        {r.name} ({r.baseCost}g · Potency {r.traits.potency} · Stab {r.traits.stability}
                        {r.traits.massDeltaKg !== 0 ? ` · Mass ${r.traits.massDeltaKg}kg` : ''}
                        {r.traits.sulphurSensitivity !== 0 ? ` · SulphurSens ${r.traits.sulphurSensitivity}` : ''})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1.5">{currentObj.description}</p>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Custom Blueprint Name & Brew Action */}
        <div className="mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={customName}
            onChange={(e) => setCustomName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={`Custom Name (default: ${previewProduct.name})`}
            className="flex-1 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
          />
          <button
            onClick={handleSynthesize}
            disabled={gold < missingCost}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            Brew Batch (+{batchUnits} Units · {missingCost}g Spot Cost)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right 5 Cols: Stoichiometric Assay Preview & Reagent Procurement */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
              02. Stoichiometric Reaction Assay
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400">
              Target: {previewProduct.targetAudience} · #{previewProduct.formulaSignature}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-100 mb-1">
              {previewProduct.name}{' '}
              <span className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400">
                [Formula #{previewProduct.formulaSignature}]
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
              Canonical ID: {previewProduct.id} · Unit Cost: {previewProduct.productionCost}g · Suggested Retail: {previewProduct.suggestedPrice}g
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-4 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Potency / Yield</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-400">{previewProduct.potency} / 100</span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Chemical Stability</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-sky-400">{previewProduct.stability} / 100</span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Hero Mass Shift</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-300">
                {previewProduct.massEffectKg === 0 ? '0kg (Normal)' : `${previewProduct.massEffectKg}kg (Featherweight)`}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Sulphur Fog Reaction</span>
              <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${previewProduct.sulphurVulnerable ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}`}>
                {previewProduct.sulphurVulnerable ? 'VULNERABLE (-55%)' : 'STABILISED'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-300 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-3">
            <div className="font-mono text-slate-400">Causal Expedition Interactions:</div>
            {previewProduct.reactionNotes.map((note, idx) => (
              <p key={idx} className="leading-relaxed text-slate-200">
                • {note}
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Wholesale Reagent Stockpile Drawer */}
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-100">
              Reagent Wholesale Exchange
            </h3>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {(['all', 'base', 'active', 'stabiliser'] as const).map((role) => (
                <button
                  key={role}
                  onClick={() => setMarketFilter(role)}
                  className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                    marketFilter === role ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {role.toUpperCase()}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
            {availableReagents
              .filter((r) => marketFilter === 'all' || r.role === marketFilter)
              .map((r) => (
                <div
                  key={r.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800/80 rounded text-xs"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-medium text-slate-200 truncate">{r.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                      {r.role} · {r.grade} · Held: {reagentInventory[r.id] || 0}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onBuyReagent(r, 3)}
                    disabled={gold < r.baseCost * 3}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-amber-300 font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 tabular-nums"
                  >
                    Buy 3 ({r.baseCost * 3}g)
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface TrapWorkshopProps {
  gold: number;
  unlockedRegions: RegionId[];
  partInventory: Record<string, number>;
  zincGuardUnlocked: boolean;
  onAssembleTrap: (product: CraftedProduct, cost: number, consumedIds: string[]) => void;
  onBuyPart: (part: TrapPart, qty: number) => void;
}

export const TrapWorkshopPanel: React.FC<TrapWorkshopProps> = ({
  gold,
  unlockedRegions,
  partInventory,
  zincGuardUnlocked,
  onAssembleTrap,
  onBuyPart
}) => {
  const availableParts = useMemo(
    () => TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.filter((p) => unlockedRegions.includes(p.regionTier)),
    [unlockedRegions]
  );

  const triggers = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'trigger');
  const concealments = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'concealment');
  const deliveries = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'delivery');
  const payloads = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'payload');
  const powers = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'power');
  const resets = availableParts.filter((p) => p.role === 'reset');

  const [triggerId, setTriggerId] = useState(triggers[0].id);
  const [concealId, setConcealId] = useState(concealments[0].id);
  const [deliveryId, setDeliveryId] = useState(deliveries[0].id);
  const [payloadId, setPayloadId] = useState(payloads[0].id);
  const [powerId, setPowerId] = useState(powers[0].id);
  const [resetId, setResetId] = useState(resets[0].id);
  const [customName, setCustomName] = useState('');
  const [partFilter, setPartFilter] = useState<TrapSlotRole | 'all'>('all');

  const selectedTuple = useMemo((): [TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart, TrapPart] => {
    const tr = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === triggerId) || triggers[0];
    const cn = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === concealId) || concealments[0];
    const dl = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === deliveryId) || deliveries[0];
    const py = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === payloadId) || payloads[0];
    const pw = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === powerId) || powers[0];
    const rs = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === resetId) || resets[0];
    return [tr, cn, dl, py, pw, rs];
  }, [triggerId, concealId, deliveryId, payloadId, powerId, resetId, triggers, concealments, deliveries, payloads, powers, resets]);

  const previewTrap = useMemo(
    () => assembleTrapProduct(selectedTuple, customName, zincGuardUnlocked),
    [selectedTuple, customName, zincGuardUnlocked]
  );

  const loadTrapPreset = (preset: 'plate_45kg' | 'sulphur_fog_machine' | 'basilisk_lair' | 'quartz_8kg_acid') => {
    if (preset === 'plate_45kg') {
      setTriggerId('part_trg_01');
      setConcealId('part_con_02');
      setDeliveryId('part_del_03');
      setPayloadId('part_pay_01');
      setPowerId('part_pow_02');
      setResetId('part_rst_02');
      setCustomName('45kg Pressure-Plate Spike Battery');
    } else if (preset === 'sulphur_fog_machine') {
      setTriggerId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_trg_07' : 'part_trg_01');
      setConcealId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_con_04' : 'part_con_02');
      setDeliveryId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_del_04' : 'part_del_02');
      setPayloadId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_pay_05' : 'part_pay_02');
      setPowerId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_pow_04' : 'part_pow_02');
      setResetId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_rst_03' : 'part_rst_02');
      setCustomName('Pneumatic Sulphur-Fog Machine');
    } else if (preset === 'basilisk_lair') {
      setTriggerId('part_trg_02');
      setConcealId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_con_03' : 'part_con_02');
      setDeliveryId('part_del_02');
      setPayloadId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_pay_04' : 'part_pay_03');
      setPowerId('part_pow_02');
      setResetId('part_rst_02');
      setCustomName('Armored Cave-Basilisk Lair Module');
    } else if (preset === 'quartz_8kg_acid') {
      setTriggerId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_trg_05' : 'part_trg_02');
      setConcealId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_con_05' : 'part_con_02');
      setDeliveryId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_del_06' : 'part_del_03');
      setPayloadId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_pay_06' : 'part_pay_03');
      setPowerId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_pow_05' : 'part_pow_02');
      setResetId(unlockedRegions.includes('warrens') ? 'part_rst_04' : 'part_rst_02');
      setCustomName('8kg Quartz-Wire Vitriol Sprayer');
    }
  };

  const missingCost = selectedTuple.reduce((acc, p) => {
    const have = partInventory[p.id] || 0;
    return acc + (have > 0 ? 0 : p.baseCost);
  }, 0);

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
      {/* Left 7 Cols: 6-Slot Modular Trap Engineering Bench */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex flex-col justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
                01. Six-Slot Modular Trap &amp; Hazard Rig
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Assemble Trigger + Concealment + Delivery + Payload + Power + Reset. Trigger mass rules govern expedition physics.
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400 tabular-nums">
              {availableParts.length} / 64 Modules Unlocked
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <p className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-2">Load Canonical Engineering Blueprint:</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => loadTrapPreset('plate_45kg')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                45kg Spike Plate (Bypassable by Chicken-Morph)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadTrapPreset('sulphur_fog_machine')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Sulphur-Fog Machine (Weakens Antidotes)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadTrapPreset('basilisk_lair')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-rose-300 rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Cave-Basilisk Petrification Lair
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => loadTrapPreset('quartz_8kg_acid')}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 rounded-md cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                8kg Hair-Trigger Acid (Catches Chicken-Morph!)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {[
              { label: '1. Trigger Sensor', list: triggers, val: triggerId, set: setTriggerId },
              { label: '2. Concealment Shroud', list: concealments, val: concealId, set: setConcealId },
              { label: '3. Delivery Mechanism', list: deliveries, val: deliveryId, set: setDeliveryId },
              { label: '4. Hazard Payload', list: payloads, val: payloadId, set: setPayloadId },
              { label: '5. Power Actuator', list: powers, val: powerId, set: setPowerId },
              { label: '6. Reset & Maintenance', list: resets, val: resetId, set: setResetId }
            ].map((slot) => {
              const currentPart = TRAP_PARTS_CATALOG.find((p) => p.id === slot.val) || slot.list[0];
              const held = partInventory[currentPart.id] || 0;
              return (
                <div key={slot.label} className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1">
                    <span>{slot.label}</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
                      Held: {held} · {currentPart.baseCost}g
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <select
                    value={slot.val}
                    onChange={(e) => slot.set(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                  >
                    {slot.list.map((p) => (
                      <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                        {p.name} ({p.baseCost}g · DC {p.detectionDC} · Leth {p.lethality}
                        {p.massThresholdKg !== undefined ? ` · >=${p.massThresholdKg}kg` : ''})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 line-clamp-2">{currentPart.description}</p>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={customName}
            onChange={(e) => setCustomName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={`Custom Blueprint Name (default: ${previewTrap.name})`}
            className="flex-1 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
          />
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (gold < missingCost) return;
              onAssembleTrap(
                previewTrap,
                missingCost,
                selectedTuple.map((p) => p.id)
              );
              setCustomName('');
            }}
            disabled={gold < missingCost}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            Assemble 2 Units ({missingCost}g Spot Cost)
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right 5 Cols: Prototype Mass & Counterplay Test Rig + Part Procurement */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
              02. Prototype Mass &amp; Counterplay Test Rig
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-rose-400">
              For Dungeon Masters · #{previewTrap.formulaSignature}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-100">
              {previewTrap.name}{' '}
              <span className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400">
                [Build #{previewTrap.formulaSignature}]
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
              Canonical ID: {previewTrap.id} · Assembly Cost: {previewTrap.productionCost}g · Suggested Contract Price: {previewTrap.suggestedPrice}g
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 mb-4 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Lethality</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-400">{previewTrap.potency}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Deterrence</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-400">{previewTrap.stability}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              <span className="text-slate-400 block">Detection DC</span>
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-sky-400">DC {previewTrap.detectionDC}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {previewTrap.recipeComponents.includes('part_rst_09') && <p className="text-sm text-amber-200 mb-3">Each purchased installation includes one temporal rewind. It prevents one wear removal, consumes that charge, then wears normally. A new paid installation replenishes it.</p>}
          {/* Mass Trigger Calibration vs Chicken-Morph Test */}
          <div className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg mb-4 text-xs">
            <div className="font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1.5">
              Trigger Calibration: {previewTrap.recipeComponents.includes('part_trg_12') ? 'Inversion: under 25kg' : `${previewTrap.triggerMassThresholdKg}kg floor threshold`}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums py-1 border-b border-slate-800/70">
              <span>Standard Vanguard (~86kg):</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{isTrapTriggeredByMass(previewTrap, 86) ? 'TRIGGERS TRAP' : 'BYPASSES SENSOR'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums py-1">
              <span>Chicken-Morphed Hero (~21kg):</span>
              <span
                className={
                  !isTrapTriggeredByMass(previewTrap, 21)
                    ? 'text-emerald-400 font-semibold'
                    : 'text-rose-400 font-semibold'
                }
              >
                {!isTrapTriggeredByMass(previewTrap, 21)
                  ? 'BYPASSES PLATE (<Threshold!)'
                  : 'TRIGGERS SENSOR'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-300">
            <div className="font-mono text-slate-400">Published Counterplay Routes ({previewTrap.counterClasses.join(' · ')}):</div>
            {previewTrap.reactionNotes.map((n, i) => (
              <p key={i} className="text-slate-300">
                • {n}
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Machine Parts Wholesale Exchange */}
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-100">
              Foundry Module Supply
            </h3>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {(['all', 'trigger', 'payload', 'reset'] as const).map((role) => (
                <button
                  key={role}
                  onClick={() => setPartFilter(role)}
                  className={`px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                    partFilter === role ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {role.toUpperCase()}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="max-h-52 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
            {availableParts
              .filter((p) => partFilter === 'all' || p.role === partFilter)
              .map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={p.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800/80 rounded text-xs"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-medium text-slate-200 truncate">{p.name}</div>
                    <div className="text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                      {p.role} · DC {p.detectionDC} · Held: {partInventory[p.id] || 0}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onBuyPart(p, 2)}
                    disabled={gold < p.baseCost * 2}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-amber-300 font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 tabular-nums"
                  >
                    Buy 2 ({p.baseCost * 2}g)
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==== END FILE src/components/CraftingPanels.tsx ====

==== BEGIN FILE src/components/TradeAndLedgerPanels.tsx BLOB 1cd96a6e416de68e7c8f4088c1aebd6d81e7caac ====
import { getDailyMarketContext } from '../engine/marketContext';
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  CraftedProduct,
  DungeonMaster,
  AdventuringParty,
  SaleContract,
  RegionId,
  FinancialRecord,
  RivalHouse
} from '../types/game';
import { REGIONS_DATA } from '../data/world';
import { computeQuotation } from '../engine/economyEngine';

interface TradeFloorProps {
  day: number;
  seed: number;
  activeRegion: RegionId;
  products: CraftedProduct[];
  masters: DungeonMaster[];
  parties: AdventuringParty[];
  scheduledMasterId: string;
  scheduledPartyId: string;
  nextMasterName: string;
  nextPartyName: string;
  contractsToday: SaleContract[];
  bilateralSkillUnlocked: boolean;
  secretShippingUnlocked: boolean;
  unlockedSkills?: string[];
  tradeTerms?: {
    priceMultiplier: number;
    warrantyBacked: boolean;
    exclusiveDeal: boolean;
    secretProvenance: boolean;
  };
  onUpdateTradeTerms?: (terms: {
    priceMultiplier: number;
    warrantyBacked: boolean;
    exclusiveDeal: boolean;
    secretProvenance: boolean;
  }) => void;
  onSelectScheduledMatchup: (masterId: string, partyId: string) => void;
  onExecuteSale: (
    buyerType: 'MASTER' | 'HERO',
    buyerId: string,
    product: CraftedProduct,
    priceMultiplier: number,
    warrantyBacked: boolean,
    exclusiveDeal: boolean,
    secretProvenance: boolean
  ) => void;
  onLaunchExpedition: () => void;
}

export const TradeFloorPanel: React.FC<TradeFloorProps> = ({
  day,
  seed,
  activeRegion,
  products,
  masters,
  parties,
  scheduledMasterId,
  scheduledPartyId,
  nextMasterName,
  nextPartyName,
  contractsToday,
  bilateralSkillUnlocked,
  secretShippingUnlocked,
  unlockedSkills = [],
  tradeTerms,
  onUpdateTradeTerms,
  onSelectScheduledMatchup,
  onExecuteSale,
  onLaunchExpedition
}) => {
  const regionMasters = masters.filter((m) => m.region === activeRegion);
  const regionParties = parties.filter((p) => p.region === activeRegion);

  // F08 Fix: Initialize from persisted tradeTerms in gameState so switching workspaces preserves settings!
  const [pricePct, setPricePct] = useState<number>(() =>
    tradeTerms?.priceMultiplier ? Math.round(tradeTerms.priceMultiplier * 100) : 100
  );
  const [warranty, setWarranty] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    tradeTerms?.warrantyBacked !== undefined ? tradeTerms.warrantyBacked : false
  );
  const [exclusive, setExclusive] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    tradeTerms?.exclusiveDeal !== undefined ? tradeTerms.exclusiveDeal : false
  );
  const [secretMark, setSecretMark] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    tradeTerms?.secretProvenance !== undefined
      ? tradeTerms.secretProvenance
      : secretShippingUnlocked
  );

  const updateTerms = (newPrice: number, newWar: boolean, newEx: boolean, newSec: boolean) => {
    setPricePct(newPrice);
    setWarranty(newWar);
    setExclusive(newEx);
    setSecretMark(newSec);
    if (onUpdateTradeTerms) {
      onUpdateTradeTerms({
        priceMultiplier: newPrice / 100,
        warrantyBacked: newWar,
        exclusiveDeal: newEx,
        secretProvenance: newSec
      });
    }
  };

  const activeMaster =
    regionMasters.find((m) => m.id === scheduledMasterId) || regionMasters[0] || masters[0];
  const activeParty =
    regionParties.find((p) => p.id === scheduledPartyId) || regionParties[0] || parties[0];

  const masterProducts = products.filter(
    (p) => (p.targetAudience === 'MASTER' || p.targetAudience === 'BOTH') && p.stock > 0
  );
  const heroProducts = products.filter(
    (p) => (p.targetAudience === 'HERO' || p.targetAudience === 'BOTH') && p.stock > 0
  );

  const effectiveMultiplier =
    (pricePct / 100) *
    (warranty ? 1.12 : 1) *
    (exclusive ? 1.25 : 1) *
    (bilateralSkillUnlocked ? 1.1 : 1);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* F01 Fix: Explicit Scheduled Expedition Matchup & Rotation Schedule Banner */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono">
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 rounded font-semibold">
              SCHEDULED EXPEDITION (DAY {day})
            </span>
            <span className="text-slate-100 font-semibold">
              {activeParty.name} (Raiders) vs {activeMaster.name} (Lair Owner)
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
            Next Scheduled Rotation (Day {day + 1}):{' '}
            <span className="text-slate-300">
              {nextPartyName} vs {nextMasterName}
            </span>{' '}
            · Selecting a client below locks them as today’s active expedition participant.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onLaunchExpedition}
          className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-mono font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
        >
          Resolve Scheduled Raid ({activeParty.name} vs {activeMaster.name}) →
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Negotiation Terms Bar */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
            Bilateral Contract Terms · Day {day} · Market ×{getDailyMarketContext(seed, day, activeRegion).factor.toFixed(3)}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            Supply {activeMaster.name} on the left and {activeParty.name} on the right before resolving their encounter.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs font-mono">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-1">
            {[
              { label: '85% Fair', val: 85 },
              { label: '100% List', val: 100 },
              { label: '125% Premium', val: 125 }
            ].map((tier) => (
              <button
                key={tier.val}
                onClick={() => updateTerms(tier.val, warranty, exclusive, secretMark)}
                className={`min-h-[36px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  pricePct === tier.val ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {tier.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={warranty}
              onChange={(e) => updateTerms(pricePct, e.target.checked, exclusive, secretMark)}
              className="accent-amber-500"
            />
            <span>Warranty (+12% Price, Refund on Failure)</span>
          </label>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={exclusive}
              onChange={(e) => updateTerms(pricePct, warranty, e.target.checked, secretMark)}
              className="accent-amber-500"
            />
            <span>Exclusivity (+22% Price)</span>
          </label>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={secretMark}
              onChange={(e) => updateTerms(pricePct, warranty, exclusive, e.target.checked)}
              className="accent-emerald-500"
            />
            <span>Null-Wax Secret Provenance (-35g Fee)</span>
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Split Bilateral Desk: Dungeon Master vs Adventuring Party */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-stretch">
        {/* Left 6 Cols: Dungeon Master Procurement */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-6 bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono text-rose-400 block">
                  SCHEDULED DUNGEON MASTER · DEFENDER
                </span>
                <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">{activeMaster.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{activeMaster.title}</p>
              </div>
              <select
                aria-label="Scheduled Dungeon Master"
                value={activeMaster.id}
                onChange={(e) => onSelectScheduledMatchup(e.target.value, activeParty.id)}
                className="bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-700 rounded px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-200 font-mono"
              >
                {regionMasters.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                    {m.name} ({m.budget}g)
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Master Metrics */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 mb-4 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Treasury</span>
                <span className="text-amber-400 font-semibold">{activeMaster.budget}g</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Deterrence</span>
                <span className="text-rose-400 font-semibold">{activeMaster.deterrenceScore}/100</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Visitor Raid %</span>
                <span className="text-sky-400 font-semibold">{activeMaster.visitorTraffic}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Trust</span>
                <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">{activeMaster.trust}/100</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg mb-4 text-xs space-y-1">
              <div className="font-mono text-amber-300">Wanted Hazard Profile: {activeMaster.desiredTrait}</div>
              <div className="font-mono text-slate-400">
                Installed Hazards ({activeMaster.installedHazards.length}/3):{' '}
                {activeMaster.installedHazards
                  .map((hid) => products.find((p) => p.id === hid)?.name || hid)
                  .join(' · ') || 'Default Spike Plate'}
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-300">{activeMaster.memoryLog[activeMaster.memoryLog.length - 1]}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-2">
              Available Traps &amp; Monster Hazards in Stock ({masterProducts.length}):
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {masterProducts.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-4 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded text-xs text-slate-400">
                  No traps in stock. Assemble traps in the Trap Workshop first.
                </div>
              ) : (
                masterProducts.map((prod) => {
                  const quote = computeQuotation(prod, activeMaster, 'MASTER', {
                    priceMultiplier: pricePct / 100,
                    warrantyBacked: warranty,
                    exclusiveDeal: exclusive,
                    secretProvenance: secretMark,
                    day,
                    seed,
                    unlockedSkills
                  });
                  const askPrice = quote.unitPrice;
                  const canAfford = quote.canAfford;
                  const acceptable = quote.acceptable;
                  const sigLabel = prod.formulaSignature || prod.id.slice(-6).toUpperCase();
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={prod.id}
                      className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3"
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100 break-words">
                          {prod.name}{' '}
                          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-amber-400">[#{sigLabel}]</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
                          Stock: {prod.stock} · Lethality {prod.potency} · DC {prod.detectionDC} · Trigger{' '}
                          {prod.triggerMassThresholdKg}kg
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 break-words">
                          {prod.reactionNotes[0]}
                        </div>
                        {!acceptable && quote.refusalReason && (
                          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-rose-400 mt-1">
                            ⚠ {quote.refusalReason}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() =>
                          onExecuteSale(
                            'MASTER',
                            activeMaster.id,
                            prod,
                            pricePct / 100,
                            warranty,
                            exclusive,
                            secretMark
                          )
                        }
                        disabled={!acceptable}
                        title={quote.refusalReason || `Expected Trust: ${quote.expectedTrustDelta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${quote.expectedTrustDelta}`}
                        className="min-h-[40px] px-3.5 py-2 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-white text-xs font-mono font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 tabular-nums"
                      >
                        {quote.refusalReason?.startsWith('Loadout full') ? 'Full' : quote.boycotting ? 'Boycott' : !canAfford ? 'No Budget' : `Sell (${askPrice}g)`}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right 6 Cols: Adventuring Party Outfitting */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-6 bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400 block">
                  SCHEDULED ADVENTURING PARTY · CHALLENGER
                </span>
                <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">{activeParty.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  {activeParty.ethics} Ethics · &ldquo;{activeParty.motto}&rdquo;
                </p>
              </div>
              <select
                aria-label="Scheduled Adventuring Party"
                value={activeParty.id}
                onChange={(e) => onSelectScheduledMatchup(activeMaster.id, e.target.value)}
                className="bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-700 rounded px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-200 font-mono"
              >
                {regionParties.map((p) => (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                    {p.name} ({p.budget}g)
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Party Metrics */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 mb-4 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Party Gold</span>
                <span className="text-amber-400 font-semibold">{activeParty.budget}g</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Vanguard Mass</span>
                <span className="text-sky-400 font-semibold">{activeParty.members[0]?.massKg || 85}kg</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Caution</span>
                <span className="text-slate-200 font-semibold">{activeParty.caution}/100</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                <span className="text-slate-400 block">Trust</span>
                <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">{activeParty.trust}/100</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg mb-4 text-xs space-y-1">
              <div className="font-mono text-emerald-300">Wanted Countermeasure: {activeParty.desiredCounter}</div>
              <div className="font-mono text-slate-400">
                Equipped Loadout ({activeParty.equippedProducts.length}/4):{' '}
                {activeParty.equippedProducts
                  .map((pid) => products.find((p) => p.id === pid)?.name || pid)
                  .join(' · ') || 'Standard Guild Issue'}
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-300">{activeParty.memoryLog[activeParty.memoryLog.length - 1]}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-2">
              Available Tonics, Antidotes &amp; Counter-Gear in Stock ({heroProducts.length}):
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {heroProducts.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-4 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded text-xs text-slate-400">
                  No hero tonics in stock. Brew antidotes or morph draughts in the Alchemy Lab first.
                </div>
              ) : (
                heroProducts.map((prod) => {
                  const quote = computeQuotation(prod, activeParty, 'HERO', {
                    priceMultiplier: pricePct / 100,
                    warrantyBacked: warranty,
                    exclusiveDeal: exclusive,
                    secretProvenance: secretMark,
                    day,
                    seed,
                    unlockedSkills
                  });
                  const askPrice = quote.unitPrice;
                  const canAfford = quote.canAfford;
                  const acceptable = quote.acceptable;
                  const sigLabel = prod.formulaSignature || prod.id.slice(-6).toUpperCase();
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={prod.id}
                      className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3"
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100 break-words">
                          {prod.name}{' '}
                          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-400">[#{sigLabel}]</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
                          Stock: {prod.stock} · Potency {prod.potency} · Stab {prod.stability}
                          {prod.massEffectKg !== 0 ? ` · ${prod.massEffectKg}kg Mass` : ''}
                          {prod.sulphurVulnerable ? ' · VULNERABLE TO SULPHUR' : ' · SULPHUR SAFE'}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 break-words">
                          {prod.reactionNotes[0]}
                        </div>
                        {!acceptable && quote.refusalReason && (
                          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-rose-400 mt-1">
                            ⚠ {quote.refusalReason}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() =>
                          onExecuteSale(
                            'HERO',
                            activeParty.id,
                            prod,
                            pricePct / 100,
                            warranty,
                            exclusive,
                            secretMark
                          )
                        }
                        disabled={!acceptable}
                        title={quote.refusalReason || `Expected Trust: ${quote.expectedTrustDelta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${quote.expectedTrustDelta}`}
                        className="min-h-[40px] px-3.5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 text-xs font-mono font-semibold rounded-lg cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 tabular-nums"
                      >
                        {quote.refusalReason?.startsWith('Loadout full') ? 'Full' : quote.boycotting ? 'Boycott' : !canAfford ? 'No Budget' : `Sell (${askPrice}g)`}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Summary of Today's Bilateral Contracts + Launch Expedition CTA */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
          <span>
            Contracts Signed Today: <strong className="text-amber-400">{contractsToday.length}</strong>
          </span>
          <span className="mx-2">·</span>
          <span>
            Today’s Recognised Revenue:{' '}
            <strong className="text-emerald-400">
              {contractsToday.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.totalRevenue, 0).toLocaleString()}g
            </strong>
          </span>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onLaunchExpedition}
          className="min-h-[44px] px-6 py-2.5 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
        >
          Resolve Dungeon Expedition ({activeParty.name} vs {activeMaster.name})
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface FinancialsPanelProps {
  gold: number;
  debt: number;
  unlockedRegions: RegionId[];
  expertUnlocked: boolean;
  ledger: FinancialRecord[];
  rivals: RivalHouse[];
  cheapCreditUnlocked: boolean;
  onUnlockRegionCharter: (regionId: RegionId, cost: number, useBridgeLoan: boolean) => void;
  onPayDownDebt: (amount: number) => void;
}

export const FinancialsPanel: React.FC<FinancialsPanelProps> = ({
  gold,
  debt,
  unlockedRegions,
  expertUnlocked,
  ledger,
  rivals,
  cheapCreditUnlocked,
  onUnlockRegionCharter,
  onPayDownDebt
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Region Trading Charters & Bridge Financing (Prevents Softlocks) */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
              01. Regional Trading Charters &amp; Bridge Financing
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              Earlier dungeons remain active in the connected world economy after later charters unlock. Bridge loans charge {cheapCreditUnlocked ? '0.3%' : '0.5%'}/day and auto-amortize out of net profits.
            </p>
          </div>
          {debt > 0 && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="text-xs font-mono text-rose-400 tabular-nums">
                Active Bridge Debt: {debt.toLocaleString()}g
              </span>
              <button
                onClick={() => onPayDownDebt(Math.min(gold, debt))}
                disabled={gold <= 0}
