                className="min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 text-xs font-mono font-semibold rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Repay Debt ({Math.min(gold, debt).toLocaleString()}g)
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {REGIONS_DATA.map((reg) => {
            const isUnlocked = unlockedRegions.includes(reg.id);
            const canBuyCash = !reg.requiresCatalyst && gold >= reg.charterCostGold;
            const minDownPayment = Math.round(reg.charterCostGold * (cheapCreditUnlocked ? 0.2 : 0.3));
            const canBridgeLoan = !reg.requiresCatalyst && gold >= minDownPayment && !isUnlocked;

            return (
              <div
                key={reg.id}
                className={`p-4 rounded-xl border flex flex-col justify-between ${
                  isUnlocked ? 'bg-[#0B0E14] border-emerald-800/70' : 'bg-[#0B0E14] border-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 mb-1">
                    <span>
                      REALM {reg.roman} · {reg.difficulty.toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <span className={isUnlocked ? 'text-emerald-400 font-semibold' : 'text-amber-400'}>
                      {isUnlocked ? 'CHARTER ACTIVE' : reg.requiresCatalyst ? 'CATALYST GATE' : `${reg.charterCostGold.toLocaleString()}g`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100 mb-1.5">{reg.name}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3 leading-relaxed">{reg.tagline}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-800/80 space-y-2">
                  {isUnlocked ? (
                    <div className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400">
                      Connected to Daily Market Loop
                    </div>
                  ) : reg.requiresCatalyst ? (
                    <div className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-300">
                      {expertUnlocked
                        ? 'Catalyst Verified! Click to Activate.'
                        : 'Requires Hard Mode (Blackspire) + Attributable Paradox Catalyst.'}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onUnlockRegionCharter(reg.id, reg.charterCostGold, false)}
                        disabled={!canBuyCash}
                        className="min-h-[38px] w-full py-1.5 px-3 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 font-mono font-semibold text-xs rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        Buy Charter Cash ({reg.charterCostGold.toLocaleString()}g)
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onUnlockRegionCharter(reg.id, reg.charterCostGold, true)}
                        disabled={!canBridgeLoan}
                        className="min-h-[38px] w-full py-1.5 px-3 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-amber-300 font-mono text-xs rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        Bridge Loan ({minDownPayment.toLocaleString()}g Down)
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Double-Entry Accounting Ledger Table */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
            02. Merchant Profit &amp; Balance Sheet Ledger
          </h2>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">
            Profit = Recognised Sales − COGS − Overhead − Warranty Claims − Fines − Interest
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-400">
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Day</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Revenue</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">COGS</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Gross Margin</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Overhead</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Claims / Fines / Int</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Net Profit</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Ending Cash</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Debt</th>
                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Net Worth</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/70">
              {ledger.map((row) => (
                <tr key={row.day} className="hover:bg-slate-900/50">
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-200 font-semibold">Day {row.day}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-emerald-400">+{row.grossRevenue.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-300">-{row.cogs.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-sky-400">{row.grossMargin.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-400">-{row.overheadAndStorage.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-rose-400">
                    -{(row.warrantyAndClaims + row.finesAndBribes + row.financingInterest).toLocaleString()}g
                  </td>
                  <td className={`py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold ${row.netProfit >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`}>
                    {row.netProfit >= 0 ? `+${row.netProfit.toLocaleString()}g` : `${row.netProfit.toLocaleString()}g`}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-amber-300">{row.endingCash.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-rose-300">{row.debt.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-100 font-semibold">{row.netWorth.toLocaleString()}g</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Rival Merchant Houses Activity */}
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100 mb-3">
          03. Competing Rival Merchant Houses (4 Active Competitors)
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {rivals.map((riv) => (
            <div key={riv.id} className="p-3.5 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-100 text-sm">{riv.name}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-amber-400 tabular-nums">
                  Market Share: {riv.marketShare}% · Undercut: {riv.priceUndercutPct}%
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-400 mb-1.5">Specialty: {riv.specialty}</div>
              <div className="font-mono text-slate-300 bg-slate-900/90 p-2 rounded border border-slate-800">
                Latest Move: {riv.lastAction}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  MarketFactOrRumour,
  DungeonMaster,
  AdventuringParty,
  SkillNode,
  ParadoxCatalystEntry,
  ExpeditionReport,
  RegionId
} from '../types/game';
import { generatePeerCatalystCode } from '../engine/simulation';
import { Sim001AuditPanel } from './Sim001AuditPanel';

interface IntelPanelProps {
  day: number;
  gold: number;
  intelFeed: MarketFactOrRumour[];
  basiliskChainStage: number;
  blackspireUnlocked: boolean;
  blackspireResolution: 'NONE' | 'HONEST_BROKER' | 'CARTEL_SYNDICATE' | 'PARTISAN_ARMOURER' | 'CALCULATED_CHAOS';
  onResolveBasiliskDecision: (choice: 'REFORMULATE' | 'REFUND_GUILD' | 'BRIBE_GRIMTHORN' | 'BLAME_RIVAL') => void;
  onChooseBlackspireEnding: (ending: 'HONEST_BROKER' | 'CARTEL_SYNDICATE' | 'PARTISAN_ARMOURER' | 'CALCULATED_CHAOS') => void;
}

export const IntelAndStoryPanel: React.FC<IntelPanelProps> = ({
  day,
  gold,
  intelFeed,
  basiliskChainStage,
  blackspireUnlocked,
  blackspireResolution,
  onResolveBasiliskDecision,
  onChooseBlackspireEnding
}) => {
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<'ALL' | 'FACTS' | 'RUMOURS'>('ALL');

  const filteredIntel = intelFeed.filter((item) => {
    if (filterType === 'FACTS') return item.isVerifiedFact;
    if (filterType === 'RUMOURS') return !item.isVerifiedFact;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
      {/* Left 7 Cols: Timestamped Market Facts vs In-World Rumours */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-4 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
              01. Morning Intelligence &amp; Rumour Desk
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              Verified facts are audited from freight manifests; tavern rumours carry explicit confidence scores.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-1 bg-[#0B0E14] p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-800">
            {(['ALL', 'FACTS', 'RUMOURS'] as const).map((f) => (
              <button
                key={f}
                onClick={() => setFilterType(f)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
                  filterType === f ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {f}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 max-h-[520px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {filteredIntel.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="p-4 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg space-y-1.5"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">
                <span>
                  DAY {item.day} · {item.timestamp} · {item.region.toUpperCase()}
                </span>
                <span className={item.isVerifiedFact ? 'text-emerald-400 font-semibold' : 'text-amber-400'}>
                  {item.isVerifiedFact
                    ? 'VERIFIED FACT (100%)'
                    : `UNCONFIRMED RUMOUR (${item.reliabilityPct}% CONFIDENCE)`}
                </span>
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100">{item.headline}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">{item.detail}</p>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500 pt-1">
                Source Provenance: {item.source}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right 5 Cols: Emergent Story Chains (Basilisk Supply Chain & Blackspire Resolution) */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-6">
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-slate-800">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
              02. Emergent Case: The Basilisk Supply Chain
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400">
              Stage {basiliskChainStage} / 3
            </span>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed mb-4">
            Overseer Grimthorn pairs a Cave-Basilisk with a Sulphur-Fog Machine. Standard unstabilised Basilisk Antidotes sold to the Silver Stag Company lose 55% potency in sulphur vapour, triggering partial petrification and a Guild Investigation. How do you play the fallout?
          </p>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onResolveBasiliskDecision('REFORMULATE')}
              className="w-full text-left p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] hover:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="font-semibold text-emerald-400 mb-0.5">
                A. Reformulate with Calcified Quartz Salt (+2 Chelated Antidotes)
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-400">
                Synthesize Sulphur-Immune Quartz-Chelated Antidotes for Silver Stag (+15 Hero Trust, creates DM demand for 8kg Acid Traps).
              </div>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => onResolveBasiliskDecision('REFUND_GUILD')}
              disabled={gold < 180}
              className="w-full text-left p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] hover:bg-slate-900 disabled:opacity-40 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="font-semibold text-sky-400 mb-0.5">
                B. Indemnify &amp; Refund Silver Stag Guild (-180g)
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-400">
                Quashes the Guild Investigation and restores +20 Hero Trust across the Warrens.
              </div>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => onResolveBasiliskDecision('BRIBE_GRIMTHORN')}
              disabled={gold < 150}
              className="w-full text-left p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] hover:bg-slate-900 disabled:opacity-40 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="font-semibold text-amber-400 mb-0.5">
                C. Subsidy Pact with Overseer Grimthorn (-150g)
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-400">
                Grimthorn throttles his Sulphur vents to keep raids profitable (+20 DM Trust, +320g contract order).
              </div>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={() => onResolveBasiliskDecision('BLAME_RIVAL')}
              className="w-full text-left p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] hover:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="font-semibold text-rose-400 mb-0.5">
                D. Frame Rival House Vane &amp; Sons for Diluted Bile
              </div>
              <div className="text-slate-400">
                Shifts blame onto Vane &amp; Sons (-6% Rival Market Share) and boosts demand for your Charcoal Breathers.
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Central Campaign Political Resolution (Blackspire Hard Mode) */}
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-slate-800">

            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">

            </span>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed mb-3">
            The core narrative resolves in Blackspire Labyrinth (Hard) without requiring Expert. Choose your merchant house’s permanent political doctrine:
          </p>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            {[
              {
                id: 'HONEST_BROKER' as const,
                title: 'Ethical Bilateral Broker',
                desc: 'Transparent warranties & non-lethal deterrence (+Trust with both sides).'
              },
              {
                id: 'CARTEL_SYNDICATE' as const,
                title: 'Unchallenged Monopoly',
                desc: 'Corner reagent supply & fix prices across all four regions (+Margin).'
              },
              {
                id: 'PARTISAN_ARMOURER' as const,

                desc: 'Indemnified state supplier to Dungeon Masters or Guilds.'
              },
              {
                id: 'CALCULATED_CHAOS' as const,

            ].map((opt) => (

                key={opt.id}

                <div className="font-semibold mb-1">{opt.title}</div>
                <div className="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">{opt.desc}</div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          {!blackspireUnlocked && (
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-amber-400/90 mt-3">
              Note: You can pre-select your house doctrine now; full Crown ratification triggers upon unlocking Blackspire Labyrinth.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                <div className="font-mono text-slate-300 tabular-nums">

                </div>

                  Latest Memory: "{m.memoryLog[m.memoryLog.length - 1]}"

        </div>

        {/* 16 Named Adventuring Parties */}

interface SpecialisationsProps {
  skillPoints: number;
  skills: SkillNode[];
  onUnlockSkill: (skillId: string) => void;
}

export const SpecialisationsPanel: React.FC<SpecialisationsProps> = ({
  skillPoints,
  skills,
  onUnlockSkill
}) => {
  const branches: Array<'Alchemy' | 'Engineering' | 'Commerce' | 'Intelligence'> = [
    'Alchemy',
    'Engineering',
    'Commerce',
    'Intelligence'
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
            Merchant Specialisation Trees (Combinable Freely)
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            No classes are sealed. Earn +1 Skill Point per resolved expedition or milestone.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm font-mono font-semibold text-amber-400 tabular-nums">
          Available Skill Points: {skillPoints} SP
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
        {branches.map((branch) => (
          <div key={branch} className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100 pb-2 border-b border-slate-800">
              {branch} Branch
            </h3>
            {skills
              .filter((s) => s.branch === branch)
              .map((sk) => (
                <div
                  key={sk.id}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-xs flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 ${
                    sk.unlocked
                      ? 'bg-emerald-950/25 border-emerald-700/70'
                      : 'bg-[#0B0E14] border-slate-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400 mb-1">
                      <span>TIER {sk.tier}</span>
                      <span>{sk.unlocked ? 'ACTIVE' : `${sk.costPoints} SP`}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-semibold text-slate-100 mb-1">{sk.name}</div>
                    <p className="text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">{sk.description}</p>
                  </div>
                  {!sk.unlocked && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => onUnlockSkill(sk.id)}
                      disabled={skillPoints < sk.costPoints}
                      className="w-full py-1.5 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 font-mono font-semibold rounded cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Unlock ({sk.costPoints} SP)
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface ArchivesAndCatalystProps {
  day: number;
  seed: number;
  blackspireUnlocked: boolean;
  expertUnlocked: boolean;
  catalystLedger: ParadoxCatalystEntry[];
  expeditionReports: ExpeditionReport[];
  customBrewsCount: number;
  customTrapsCount: number;
  masterSalesCount: number;
  heroSalesCount: number;
  onClaimSoloDmCatalyst: () => void;
  onImportPeerCatalyst: (code: string) => { valid: boolean; message: string };
  onExportSaveJson: () => string;
  onImportSaveJson: (jsonStr: string) => boolean;
}

export const ArchivesAndCatalystPanel: React.FC<ArchivesAndCatalystProps> = ({
  day,
  seed,
  blackspireUnlocked,
  expertUnlocked,
  catalystLedger,
  expeditionReports,
  customBrewsCount,
  customTrapsCount,
  masterSalesCount,
  heroSalesCount,
  onClaimSoloDmCatalyst,
  onImportPeerCatalyst,
  onExportSaveJson,
  onImportSaveJson
}) => {
  const [subView, setSubView] = useState<'SIM_001' | 'CATALYST_SAVES'>('SIM_001');
  const [peerInputCode, setPeerInputCode] = useState('');
  const [peerFeedback, setPeerFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saveJsonText, setSaveJsonText] = useState('');
  const [saveFeedback, setSaveFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const outboundShareCode = generatePeerCatalystCode('HOUSE_MERCHANT', day);
  const sampleValidPeerCode = generatePeerCatalystCode('VESPERA_ENVOY', day + 1);

  const bilateralVerified = masterSalesCount >= 2 && heroSalesCount >= 2;
  const soloCommissionReady =
    blackspireUnlocked &&
    customBrewsCount >= 2 &&
    customTrapsCount >= 2 &&
    bilateralVerified;

  const handlePeerSubmit = () => {
    const res = onImportPeerCatalyst(peerInputCode);
    setPeerFeedback(res.message);
    if (res.valid) setPeerInputCode('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
          ARCHIVES &amp; INDEPENDENT AUDIT WORKSPACE (SIM-001 BASELINE &amp; SIM-002 REPAIR)
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-1 bg-[#0B0E14] p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-800">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSubView('SIM_001')}
            className={`min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
              subView === 'SIM_001'
                ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            SIM-001 &amp; SIM-002 Empirical Audit (5,000 Campaigns)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSubView('CATALYST_SAVES')}
            className={`min-h-[38px] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
              subView === 'CATALYST_SAVES'
                ? 'bg-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-semibold'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            Paradox Catalyst Gate &amp; Save Ledger
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {subView === 'SIM_001' ? (
        <Sim001AuditPanel />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
      {/* Left 7 Cols: Paradox Catalyst Gate & Provenance Gift Ledger */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 mb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
                01. The Paradox Catalyst Gate (Hard → Expert)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Hard Rule: Requires prior Blackspire Labyrinth (Hard) access + an attributable Catalyst via Vespera’s Commission or Cryptographic Peer Exchange.
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className={`text-xs font-mono font-semibold ${expertUnlocked ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-amber-400'}`}>
              {expertUnlocked ? 'THE PARADOX VAULT UNLOCKED' : 'AWAITING HARD CHARTER & CATALYST'}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Route A: Solo Self-Found Offline DM Commission */}
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg mb-4">
            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-400 mb-1">
              ROUTE A · SOLO OFFLINE DUNGEON MASTER COMMISSION (Arch-Architect Vespera)
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mb-3">
              Satisfy Vespera’s four-part Hard-mode engineering, alchemy, and bilateral supply audit to receive a couriered Paradox Catalyst:
            </p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums mb-3">
              <div className={`p-2 rounded border ${blackspireUnlocked ? 'border-emerald-700 text-emerald-400' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-400'}`}>
                1. Hard Mode (Blackspire): {blackspireUnlocked ? 'UNLOCKED' : 'LOCKED'}
              </div>
              <div className={`p-2 rounded border ${customBrewsCount >= 2 ? 'border-emerald-700 text-emerald-400' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-400'}`}>
                2. Custom Alchemy Brews: {customBrewsCount}/2
              </div>
              <div className={`p-2 rounded border ${customTrapsCount >= 2 ? 'border-emerald-700 text-emerald-400' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-400'}`}>
                3. Custom Trap Assemblies: {customTrapsCount}/2
              </div>
              <div className={`p-2 rounded border ${bilateralVerified ? 'border-emerald-700 text-emerald-400' : 'border-slate-800 text-slate-400'}`}>
                4. Bilateral Contracts: {masterSalesCount}/2 DM · {heroSalesCount}/2 Hero
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={onClaimSoloDmCatalyst}
              disabled={!soloCommissionReady || expertUnlocked}
              className="min-h-[42px] px-4 py-2 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-500 text-slate-950 font-mono font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
            >
              {expertUnlocked
                ? 'Catalyst Recorded in Gift Ledger'
                : soloCommissionReady
                ? 'Receive Couriered Paradox Catalyst from Arch-Architect Vespera'
                : 'Complete All 4 Hard-Mode Commission Requirements Above'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Route B: Community Merchant Dispatch Code Exchange */}
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded-lg">
            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-sky-400 mb-1">
              ROUTE B · COMMUNITY MERCHANT DISPATCH CODE (Format Checksum &amp; Replay Protection)
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3">
              Once Blackspire Labyrinth (Hard) is unlocked, enter a community peer dispatch code. Codes are verified for format syntax and local replay prevention. Note: this is a casual community trade code; authentic offline progression is earned via Vespera’s Solo Commission above.
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded text-xs font-mono mb-3">
              <span className="text-slate-300">Your Outbound Community Dispatch Code:</span>
              <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold select-all">{outboundShareCode}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 mb-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={peerInputCode}
                onChange={(e) => setPeerInputCode(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter Community Dispatch Code (CATALYST-...)"
                className="flex-1 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono text-slate-100"
              />
              <button
                onClick={handlePeerSubmit}
                className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 bg-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-500 text-slate-950 font-mono font-semibold text-xs rounded cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
              >
                Verify &amp; Record Catalyst
              </button>
            </div>
            {peerFeedback && (
              <div className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-300 mt-1">{peerFeedback}</div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Gift Ledger History */}
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100 mb-3">
            02. Attributable Paradox Catalyst Gift Ledger ({catalystLedger.length})
          </h3>
          {catalystLedger.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
              No Paradox Catalysts recorded yet. Complete Vespera’s Solo Commission or verify a Peer Code above.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {catalystLedger.map((entry) => (
                <div key={entry.id} className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-emerald-800/70 rounded text-xs font-mono">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                    <span>Sender: {entry.senderName} ({entry.senderType})</span>
                    <span>Hash: #{entry.provenanceHash} · Day {entry.dayReceived}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-slate-300 mt-1">{entry.commissionNote}</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right 5 Cols: Seeded Deterministic Replay Log & Atomic Save Backup Export/Import */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100 mb-2">
            03. Atomic Save Export &amp; Restore
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-3">
            Game state auto-saves atomically after every action and on app visibility change. Export or restore a full JSON snapshot anytime.
          </p>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                const dumped = onExportSaveJson();
                setSaveJsonText(dumped);
                setSaveFeedback('Snapshot exported to text box below.');
              }}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer"
            >
              Export Save JSON
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                const ok = onImportSaveJson(saveJsonText);
                setSaveFeedback(ok ? 'Save snapshot restored successfully!' : 'Invalid JSON snapshot.');
              }}
              disabled={!saveJsonText.trim()}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-xs font-mono rounded cursor-pointer"
            >
              Import Save JSON
            </button>
          </div>
          <textarea
            value={saveJsonText}
            onChange={(e) => setSaveJsonText(e.target.value)}
            rows={4}
            placeholder="Click 'Export Save JSON' or paste a saved campaign JSON string here..."
            className="w-full bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded p-2.5 text-xs font-mono text-slate-300"
          />
          {saveFeedback && <div className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400 mt-1.5">{saveFeedback}</div>}
        </div>

        {/* Deterministic Seeded Expedition Replay Archive */}
        <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
            <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-100">
              04. Deterministic Replay Archive
            </h3>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
              Campaign Seed: #{seed}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
            {expeditionReports.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded">
                No expeditions resolved yet. Complete a trade day to record seeded causal replays.
              </div>
            ) : (
              expeditionReports.map((rep) => (
                <div key={rep.id} className="p-3 bg-[#0B0E14] border border-slate-800 rounded text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-slate-400 mb-1">
                    <span>Day {rep.day} · {rep.region.toUpperCase()}</span>
                    <span>Seed #{rep.seed} · {rep.rulesVersion}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="font-semibold text-slate-100 mb-1">{rep.summaryHeadline}</div>
                  <div className="text-slate-400 font-mono">
                    Loot: {rep.totalLooted}g · Master Δ: {rep.masterTreasuryDelta >= 0 ? `+${rep.masterTreasuryDelta}g` : `${rep.masterTreasuryDelta}g`}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ExpeditionReport, ExpeditionRoomEvent } from '../types/game';
import { soundFX } from '../utils/sound';

interface ExpeditionCanvasProps {
  mode: 'TITLE_BACKDROP' | 'EXPEDITION_LIVE';
  report: ExpeditionReport | null;
  activeRoomIdx: number;
  simSpeed: 1 | 2 | 4;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  onRoomAdvance?: (nextIdx: number) => void;
}

interface Particle {
  active: boolean;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  size: number;
  alpha: number;
  decay: number;
  color: string;
}

export const ExpeditionCanvas: React.FC<ExpeditionCanvasProps> = React.memo(({
  mode,
  report,
  activeRoomIdx,
  simSpeed,
  reducedMotion,
  onRoomAdvance
}) => {
  const [camera, setCamera] = useState({ x:0, y:0 });
  const cameraRef = useRef(camera);
  const moveCamera = (x:number,y:number) => {
    const next = { x:Math.max(-284,Math.min(284,x)), y:Math.max(-50,Math.min(50,y)) };
    cameraRef.current=next; setCamera(next);
  };
  const drag = useRef<{id:number;x:number;y:number} | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => { moveCamera((1-activeRoomIdx)*284,0); },[activeRoomIdx,report?.id]);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const propsRef = useRef({ mode, report, activeRoomIdx, simSpeed, reducedMotion, onRoomAdvance });

  useEffect(() => {
    propsRef.current = { mode, report, activeRoomIdx, simSpeed, reducedMotion, onRoomAdvance };
  }, [mode, report, activeRoomIdx, simSpeed, reducedMotion, onRoomAdvance]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    // Input state: separate continuous held keys from edge-triggered justPressed
    const heldKeys = new Set<string>();
    const justPressed = new Set<string>();

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes((e.target as HTMLElement)?.tagName)) return;
      const code = e.code;
      if (!heldKeys.has(code)) {
        justPressed.add(code);
      }
      heldKeys.add(code);
    };

    const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      heldKeys.delete(e.code);
    };

    const onBlur = () => {
      heldKeys.clear();
      justPressed.clear();
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    window.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);

    // Pooled particles (120 max, zero GC allocations in loop)
    const POOL_SIZE = 120;
    const particles: Particle[] = Array.from({ length: POOL_SIZE }, () => ({
      active: false,
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      vx: 0,
      vy: 0,
      size: 3,
      alpha: 0,
      decay: 1,
      color: '#D97706'
    }));

    const spawnBurst = (x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speedSpread: number) => {
      let spawned = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < POOL_SIZE && spawned < count; i++) {
        if (!particles[i].active) {
          const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
          const speed = (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7) * speedSpread;
          particles[i].active = true;
          particles[i].x = x;
          particles[i].y = y;
          particles[i].vx = Math.cos(angle) * speed;
          particles[i].vy = Math.sin(angle) * speed - 15;
          particles[i].size = 2 + Math.random() * 4;
          particles[i].alpha = 1;
          particles[i].decay = 0.65 + Math.random() * 0.6;
          particles[i].color = color;
          spawned++;
        }
      }
    };

    // Simulation state inside loop
    let animFrameId = 0;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    let shakeIntensity = 0;
    let hitstopTimer = 0;
    let hitFlashAlpha = 0;
    let roomPhaseTimer = 0;
    let lastTriggeredRoom = -1;
    let heroSquashX = 1;
    let heroSquashY = 1;

    const resize = () => {
      if (!canvas.parentElement) return;
      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      canvas.width = canvas.parentElement.clientWidth * dpr;
      canvas.height = canvas.parentElement.clientHeight * dpr;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };
    resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

    const loop = (now: number) => {
      const rawDt = (now - lastTime) / 1000;
      lastTime = now;
      const dt = Math.min(rawDt, 0.05) * propsRef.current.simSpeed;

      const width = canvas.clientWidth || 800;
      const height = canvas.clientHeight || 500;
      const { mode: curMode, report: curReport, activeRoomIdx: curRoomIdx, reducedMotion: isReduced } = propsRef.current;

      // Edge-triggered Space action to step room or trigger burst
      if (justPressed.has('Space') && curMode === 'EXPEDITION_LIVE' && curReport) {
        if (propsRef.current.onRoomAdvance) {
          const next = (curRoomIdx + 1) % curReport.rooms.length;
          propsRef.current.onRoomAdvance(next);
        }
      }
      justPressed.clear();

      // Continuous WASD camera inspection offset
      const panSpeed = 140 * dt;
      let {x:camPanX,y:camPanY}=cameraRef.current;
      if (heldKeys.has('KeyA') || heldKeys.has('ArrowLeft')) camPanX -= panSpeed;
      if (heldKeys.has('KeyD') || heldKeys.has('ArrowRight')) camPanX += panSpeed;
      if (heldKeys.has('KeyW') || heldKeys.has('ArrowUp')) camPanY -= panSpeed;
      if (heldKeys.has('KeyS') || heldKeys.has('ArrowDown')) camPanY += panSpeed;
      if (camPanX !== cameraRef.current.x || camPanY !== cameraRef.current.y) moveCamera(camPanX,camPanY);
      camPanX=cameraRef.current.x; camPanY=cameraRef.current.y;

      // Handle brief 30-80ms simulation hitstop on heavy impacts
      if (hitstopTimer > 0) {
        hitstopTimer -= rawDt;
        animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        return;
      }

      roomPhaseTimer += dt;
      if (curRoomIdx !== lastTriggeredRoom) {
        lastTriggeredRoom = curRoomIdx;
        roomPhaseTimer = 0;
        const roomEvt: ExpeditionRoomEvent | undefined = curReport?.rooms[curRoomIdx];
        if (roomEvt && curMode === 'EXPEDITION_LIVE') {
          if (roomEvt.outcome === 'TRAP_TRIGGERED' || roomEvt.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE') {
            hitstopTimer = isReduced ? 0 : 0.055; // 55ms hitstop
            shakeIntensity = isReduced ? 0 : roomEvt.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE' ? 11 : 7;
            hitFlashAlpha = isReduced ? 0 : 0.28;
            heroSquashX = 1.28;
            heroSquashY = 0.74;
            soundFX.playImpact(roomEvt.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE');
            spawnBurst(
              width * 0.52,
              height * 0.54,
              roomEvt.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE' ? '#F59E0B' : '#F43F5E',
              28,
              95
            );
          } else if (roomEvt.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED' || roomEvt.outcome === 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED') {
            heroSquashX = 0.86;
            heroSquashY = 1.18;
            soundFX.playBrew();
            spawnBurst(width * 0.52, height * 0.52, '#10B981', 22, 75);
          }
        }
      }

      // Decay squash & shake
      heroSquashX += (1 - heroSquashX) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
      heroSquashY += (1 - heroSquashY) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
      shakeIntensity *= Math.pow(0.04, dt);
      hitFlashAlpha = Math.max(0, hitFlashAlpha - dt * 1.8);

      const shakeX = isReduced ? 0 : (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * shakeIntensity;
      const shakeY = isReduced ? 0 : (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * shakeIntensity;

      // Clear deep obsidian background
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0B0E14';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(camPanX + shakeX, camPanY + shakeY);

      // Subtle architectural subterranean masonry grid
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.06)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      const gridStep = 48;
      const slowPan = curMode === 'TITLE_BACKDROP' && !isReduced ? (now * 0.012) % gridStep : 0;
      for (let gx = -gridStep; gx < width + gridStep; gx += gridStep) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(gx - slowPan, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(gx - slowPan, height);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let gy = 0; gy < height; gy += gridStep) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, gy);
        ctx.lineTo(width, gy);
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      if (curMode === 'TITLE_BACKDROP') {
        // Atmospheric Title Screen Live World Cutaway: Twin alchemical condensers + subterranean dungeon corridor
        const cy = height * 0.55;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#111622';
        ctx.fillRect(0, cy + 40, width, height - cy);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(217, 119, 6, 0.25)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, cy + 40);
        ctx.lineTo(width, cy + 40);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Ambient floating alchemical & sulphur motes
        if (Math.random() < 0.35 && !isReduced) {
          spawnBurst(
            width * (0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6),
            cy + 20,
            Math.random() > 0.5 ? '#D97706' : '#10B981',
            1,
            24
          );
        }
      } else if (curReport) {
        // Render 3-Room Dungeon Cross-Section
        const floorY = height * 0.68;
        const roomW = 260;
        const startX = (width - roomW * 3 - 48) / 2;

        // Corridor floor
        ctx.fillStyle = '#131926';
        ctx.fillRect(startX - 24, floorY, roomW * 3 + 96, 18);

        curReport.rooms.forEach((rm, idx) => {
          const rx = startX + idx * (roomW + 24);
          const ry = floorY - 170;
          const isCurrent = idx === curRoomIdx;

          // Room chamber frame
          ctx.fillStyle = isCurrent ? '#161F30' : '#0F1420';
          ctx.strokeStyle = isCurrent
            ? rm.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE'
              ? '#F59E0B'
              : rm.outcome === 'TRAP_TRIGGERED'
              ? '#F43F5E'
              : '#10B981'
            : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.16)';
          ctx.lineWidth = isCurrent ? 2 : 1;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.roundRect(rx, ry, roomW, 170, 8);
          ctx.fill();
          ctx.stroke();

          // Sulphur Fog visual haze inside room if present
          if (rm.chemicalReaction?.includes('Sulphur') || rm.hazardName.includes('Sulphur')) {
            const pulse = isReduced ? 0.16 : 0.14 + Math.sin(now * 0.004 + idx) * 0.05;
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(234, 179, 8, ${pulse})`;
            ctx.fillRect(rx + 6, ry + 34, roomW - 12, 128);
          }

          // Room Header
          ctx.fillStyle = '#94A3B8';
          ctx.font = '600 11px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';
          ctx.fillText(`ROOM 0${rm.roomIndex} · ${rm.triggerType}`, rx + 12, ry + 22, roomW-24);

          // Pressure Plate / Trigger Telegraph on Floor
          const plateX = rx + roomW * 0.55;
          const plateDepressed = rm.outcome === 'TRAP_TRIGGERED' || rm.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE';
          ctx.fillStyle = plateDepressed ? '#F43F5E' : rm.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED' ? '#10B981' : '#D97706';
          ctx.fillRect(plateX - 24, floorY - (plateDepressed ? 3 : 7), 48, plateDepressed ? 3 : 7);

          // Windup telegraph arc
          if (isCurrent) {
            const windupPct = Math.min(1, roomPhaseTimer / 1.2);
            ctx.strokeStyle = plateDepressed ? 'rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.55)' : 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.55)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(plateX, floorY - 48, 26, -Math.PI * 0.8, -Math.PI * 0.8 + Math.PI * 1.6 * windupPct);
            ctx.stroke();
          }

          // Hazard mechanism icon/silhouette
          ctx.fillStyle = '#E2E8F0';
          ctx.font = '600 12px "Plus Jakarta Sans", sans-serif';
          const shortHazard = rm.hazardName.length > 26 ? rm.hazardName.slice(0, 25) + '…' : rm.hazardName;
          ctx.fillText(shortHazard, rx + 12, ry + 46);

          // Mass readout vs Threshold
          if (rm.triggerMassThresholdKg > 0) {
            ctx.fillStyle = rm.heroEffectiveMassKg < rm.triggerMassThresholdKg ? '#34D399' : '#FBBF24';
            ctx.font = '500 11px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';
            ctx.fillText(
              `Hero ${rm.heroEffectiveMassKg}kg vs Plate ${rm.triggerMassThresholdKg}kg`,
              rx + 12,
              ry + 66
            );
          }

          // Hero Squad Avatar with Squash-and-Stretch & Chicken-Morph visual indicator
          const isChickenMorphed = rm.heroEffectiveMassKg <= 30;
          const hx = rx + roomW * 0.28;
          const hy = floorY - 8;

          ctx.save();
          ctx.translate(hx, hy);
          ctx.scale(isCurrent ? heroSquashX : 1, isCurrent ? heroSquashY : 1);

          if (isChickenMorphed) {
            // Draw nimble Featherweight / Bantam Rooster silhouette
            ctx.fillStyle = '#FDE68A';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(0, -14, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
            // Red comb
            ctx.fillStyle = '#F43F5E';
            ctx.fillRect(-3, -29, 6, 5);
            // Beak
            ctx.fillStyle = '#D97706';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(10, -16);
            ctx.lineTo(17, -13);
            ctx.lineTo(10, -10);
            ctx.fill();
          } else {
            // Armored Hero Vanguard silhouette
            ctx.fillStyle = '#38BDF8';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.roundRect(-12, -38, 24, 34, 5);
            ctx.fill();
            // Shield
            ctx.fillStyle = '#94A3B8';
            ctx.fillRect(6, -32, 9, 22);
          }
          ctx.restore();

          // Outcome label inside room bottom
          const badgeColor =
            rm.outcome === 'TRAP_BYPASSED' || rm.outcome === 'COUNTER_SUCCEEDED'
              ? '#10B981'
              : rm.outcome === 'REACTION_FAILURE'
              ? '#F59E0B'
              : '#F43F5E';
          ctx.fillStyle = badgeColor;
          ctx.font = '700 11px "JetBrains Mono", monospace';
          ctx.fillText(
            `${rm.outcome.replace('_', ' ')} · -${rm.damageDealt}HP · +${rm.lootSecured}g`,
            rx + 12,
            ry + 154
          );
        });
      }

      // Update & draw pooled particles
      for (let i = 0; i < POOL_SIZE; i++) {
        const p = particles[i];
        if (!p.active) continue;
        p.x += p.vx * dt;
        p.y += p.vy * dt;
        p.vy += 38 * dt;
        p.alpha -= p.decay * dt;
        if (p.alpha <= 0) {
          p.active = false;
          continue;
        }
        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.alpha);
        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.restore();

      // Hit flash overlay
      if (hitFlashAlpha > 0) {
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(245, 158, 11, ${hitFlashAlpha})`;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
      }

      animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };

    animFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animFrameId);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
      window.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full h-full overflow-hidden bg-[#0B0E14]">
      <canvas
        aria-label="Dungeon expedition camera. Drag to pan; arrow keys or WASD also pan."
        tabIndex={mode === 'EXPEDITION_LIVE' ? 0 : -1}
        data-camera-x={camera.x.toFixed(2)} data-camera-y={camera.y.toFixed(2)}
        onPointerDown={e => { if (mode !== 'EXPEDITION_LIVE') return; e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); drag.current={id:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY}; }}
        onPointerMove={e => { const d=drag.current; if (!d || d.id!==e.pointerId) return;
          moveCamera(cameraRef.current.x+e.clientX-d.x,cameraRef.current.y+e.clientY-d.y);
          drag.current={id:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY}; }}
        onPointerUp={() => { drag.current=null; }} onPointerCancel={() => { drag.current=null; }}
        onLostPointerCapture={() => { drag.current=null; }}
        ref={canvasRef}
        className="block w-full h-full max-w-full"
        style={{ touchAction: mode === 'EXPEDITION_LIVE' ? 'none' : 'pan-y' }}
      />
    </div>
  );
});

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { ExpeditionCanvas } from './ExpeditionCanvas';

interface TitleScreenProps {
  hasSavedGame: boolean;
  savedDay: number;
  savedGold: number;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  onStartNewOrContinue: () => void;
  onLoadBasiliskScenario: () => void;
  onResetSave: () => void;
}

export const TitleScreen: React.FC<TitleScreenProps> = ({
  hasSavedGame,
  savedDay,
  savedGold,
  reducedMotion,
  onStartNewOrContinue,
  onLoadBasiliskScenario,
  onResetSave
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onStartNewOrContinue();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKey);
  }, [onStartNewOrContinue]);

  return (
    <div className="relative w-screen h-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#0B0E14] select-none flex flex-col justify-between p-8 md:p-14">
      {/* Live 60fps Atmospheric Dungeon & Alchemy Cutaway Canvas */}

          mode="TITLE_BACKDROP"
          report={null}
          activeRoomIdx={0}
          simSpeed={1}

        />
      </div>

      {/* Measured contrast scrim for guaranteed WCAG AA legibility */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#0B0E14] via-[#0B0E14]/65 to-[#0B0E14]/40 pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Top quiet edition metadata (unboxed text, zero pills) */}

      </div>

      {/* Centerpiece: Bespoke SVG Lockup over generous negative space */}
      <div className="relative z-10 max-w-3xl mx-auto text-center my-auto py-8">
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <svg
            width="128"
            height="92"
            viewBox="0 0 128 92"
            fill="none"
            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
            aria-label="Trap and Tonic Balance Scale Emblem"
            className="text-amber-500"
          >
            {/* Central Merchant Balance Beam */}
            <path d="M64 10V78M42 78H86" stroke="#D97706" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
            <path d="M20 26L64 18L108 26" stroke="#F59E0B" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" />
            <circle cx="64" cy="18" r="4.5" fill="#FDE68A" />
            {/* Left Pan: Alchemical Tonic Flask (Emerald) */}
            <path d="M20 26L12 46H28L20 26Z" stroke="#94A3B8" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            <path
              d="M16 38H24V43L29 53C30 55 28.5 58 26 58H14C11.5 58 10 55 11 53L16 43V38Z"
              fill="#064E3B"
              stroke="#10B981"
              strokeWidth="2"
            />
            <circle cx="19" cy="51" r="2" fill="#34D399" />
            <circle cx="23" cy="48" r="1.5" fill="#A7F3D0" />
            {/* Right Pan: Serrated Dungeon Trap Jaw (Crimson/Brass) */}
            <path d="M108 26L100 46H116L108 26Z" stroke="#94A3B8" strokeWidth="1.5" />
            <path
              d="M95 56C95 48 101 44 108 44C115 44 121 48 121 56H95Z"
              fill="#4C0519"
              stroke="#F43F5E"
              strokeWidth="2"
            />
            <path d="M99 48L102 53L105 47L108 53L111 47L114 53L117 48" stroke="#FDA4AF" strokeWidth="1.5" />
          </svg>
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs font-mono tracking-widest text-amber-400/90 mb-3">
          THE HOUSE ALWAYS SELLS TWICE
        </p>

        <h1 className="font-display text-4xl sm:text-6xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-50 mb-4">
          Trap &amp; Tonic: Conflict of Interest
        </h1>

        <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-300 max-w-xl mx-auto leading-relaxed mb-8">
          Supply Dungeon Masters with lethal pressure plates, basilisks, and sulphur fog by morning—then sell Adventuring Parties the featherweight morphs and chelated antidotes to survive them by night.
        </p>

        {/* Primary CTA & Scenario Quick-Load */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4">
          <button
            onClick={onStartNewOrContinue}
            className="px-7 py-3.5 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 shadow-lg"
          >
            {hasSavedGame ? `Continue Ledger (Day ${savedDay} · ${savedGold.toLocaleString()}g)` : 'Open Merchant Ledger'}
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={onLoadBasiliskScenario}
            className="px-5 py-3.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 font-medium text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            Load Scenario: The Basilisk Supply Chain
          </button>

          {hasSavedGame && (
            <button
              onClick={onResetSave}
              className="px-4 py-3.5 text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Reset Campaign
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Single flat control-hint line at bottom */}
      <div className="relative z-10 text-center text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">
        <span>Enter / Space Open Ledger · WASD Pan Dungeon Camera · Space Step Room Replay · Esc Pause &amp; Save</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==== BEGIN FILE src/components/PWAInstallWidgets.tsx BLOB 54e79e0f0b57a7517ff1cd4ec97e28258b8a74fe ====
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

export function usePWAInstall() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const isStandalone =
      window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
      (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
    setIsInstalled(isStandalone);

    const userAgent = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    const isIOSDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(userAgent);
    setIsIOS(isIOSDevice);

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    };
  }, []);

  const install = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return false;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (outcome === 'accepted') {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS,
    install
  };
}

export function useOnlineStatus() {
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(
    typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOnline = () => setIsOnline(true);
    const handleOffline = () => setIsOnline(false);

    window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener('offline', handleOffline);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener('offline', handleOffline);
    };
  }, []);

  return isOnline;
}

export const PWAInstallButton: React.FC = () => {
  const { isInstallable, isInstalled, isIOS, install } = usePWAInstall();
  const [showIOSGuide, setShowIOSGuide] = useState(false);

  if (isInstalled) {
    return null;
  }

  if (isInstallable) {
    return (
      <button
        onClick={install}
        className="min-h-[40px] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-emerald-300 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-emerald-700/70 rounded-lg transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
      >
        Install Offline App
      </button>
    );
  }

  if (isIOS) {
    return (
      <>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowIOSGuide(true)}
          className="min-h-[40px] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono font-medium text-emerald-300 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
        >
          Install on iOS
        </button>

        {showIOSGuide && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 p-4">
            <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-xl bg-[#131822] border border-slate-700 p-6 shadow-xl">
              <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-100">
                Install Offline on iPhone / iPad
              </h3>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                1. Tap the <strong>Share</strong> button in the Safari toolbar.<br />
                2. Scroll down and tap <strong>Add to Home Screen</strong> for full offline cold-start play.
              </p>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowIOSGuide(false)}
                className="mt-4 w-full min-h-[44px] rounded-lg bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-slate-950 cursor-pointer"
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }

  return null;
};

export const OfflineIndicator: React.FC = () => {
  const isOnline = useOnlineStatus();

  if (isOnline) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-16 md:bottom-4 left-4 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-600 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-slate-950 shadow-lg">
      <span>OFFLINE COLD-START ACTIVE — Local Service Worker &amp; Atomic Ledger in Use</span>
    </div>
  );
};

    metrics: CampaignMetrics;

                        {st.terminalNetWorth.p50.toLocaleString()}g
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right text-rose-300">
                        {st.totalClaimsAndFines.p50.toLocaleString()}g
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                        <span
                          className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] ${
                            st.strictBankruptcyRatePct > 0
                              ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/30'
                              : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {st.strictBankruptcyRatePct}%
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right text-slate-300">
                        {st.bailoutFloorTriggeredDays.p50}d
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right text-slate-300">
                        {st.normalUnlockRatePct}% (D{st.dayReachedNormal.p50 ?? '—'}) /{' '}
                        {st.hardUnlockRatePct}% (D{st.dayReachedHard.p50 ?? '—'})
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right text-slate-300">
                        {st.expertCatalystStrictRatePct}% / {st.expertCatalystUiRuleRatePct}%
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 pl-2 text-right text-sky-300">
                        {st.partySurvivalRatePct.p50}%
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TAB 2: DISTRIBUTIONS & PERCENTILES */}
      {activeSubTab === 'DISTRIBUTIONS' && (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {strategiesOrder.map((stKey) => {
            const st = data.strategies[stKey];
            const info = STRATEGY_LABELS[stKey];
            const metricsList = [
              { label: 'Gross Revenue (g)', obj: st.grossRevenue },
              { label: 'Total COGS (g)', obj: st.totalCogs },
              { label: 'Total Overhead (g)', obj: st.totalOverhead },
              { label: 'Claims & Fines (g)', obj: st.totalClaimsAndFines },
              { label: 'Loan Interest Paid (g)', obj: st.totalInterestPaid },
              { label: 'Net Profit (g)', obj: st.netProfit },
              { label: 'Terminal Net Worth (g)', obj: st.terminalNetWorth },
              { label: 'Min Raw Cash Before Floor (g)', obj: st.minRawCashBeforeFloor },
              { label: 'Dungeon Master Trust (0-100)', obj: st.masterAvgTrust },
              { label: 'Hero Party Trust (0-100)', obj: st.heroAvgTrust },
              { label: 'Party Survival Rate (%)', obj: st.partySurvivalRatePct },
              { label: 'Traps Triggered (Rooms)', obj: st.trapTriggeredCount },
              { label: 'Mass Pressure Bypasses (Rooms)', obj: st.trapBypassedMassCount },
              { label: 'Sulphur Reaction Failures', obj: st.reactionFailureSulphurCount }

            return (
              <div
                key={stKey}

                      {info.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{info.subtitle}</p>
                  </div>

                    1,000 Campaigns • Normal: {st.normalUnlockRatePct}% • Hard:{' '}
                    {st.hardUnlockRatePct}% • Expert: {st.expertCatalystStrictRatePct}%
                  </div>
                </div>

                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-400 text-[10px] uppercase">
                      <th className="py-2 pr-3">Metric</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">Mean</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">Min</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">P10</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">P25</th>

                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">P75</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">P90</th>
                      <th className="py-2 pl-2 text-right">Max</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/50">
                    {metricsList.map((m) => (

      )}

      )}

          {liveResult && (
            <div className="bg-[#131822] border border-emerald-500/40 rounded-xl p-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-slate-800">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-emerald-400">
                    LIVE DETERMINISTIC EXECUTION COMPLETE
                  </span>

                    Campaign {liveResult.metrics.campaignId} ({liveResult.metrics.strategy})
                  </h4>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">
                  Actions Logged: {liveResult.actionLogCount} • Rooms Resolved:{' '}
                  {liveResult.metrics.totalRoomsSimulated}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs font-mono">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">GROSS REVENUE</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-100 mt-0.5">
                    {liveResult.metrics.grossRevenue.toLocaleString()}g
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">NET PROFIT</div>
                  <div
                    className={`text-sm font-bold mt-0.5 ${
                      liveResult.metrics.netProfit >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {liveResult.metrics.netProfit.toLocaleString()}g
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">TERMINAL NET WORTH</div>
                  <div
                    className={`text-sm font-bold mt-0.5 ${
                      liveResult.metrics.terminalNetWorth >= 0 ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-rose-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {liveResult.metrics.terminalNetWorth.toLocaleString()}g
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">BAILOUT FLOOR DAYS</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">
                    {liveResult.metrics.bailoutFloorTriggeredDays} days (Min Cash:{' '}
                    {liveResult.metrics.minRawCashBeforeFloor}g)
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-2">
                    Milestone & Tactical Summary
                  </div>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
                    <li>• Day Reached Normal: {liveResult.metrics.dayReachedNormal ?? 'Never'}</li>
                    <li>• Day Reached Hard: {liveResult.metrics.dayReachedHard ?? 'Never'}</li>
                    <li>
                      • Day Reached Expert (Hard-Gated F03):{' '}
                      {liveResult.metrics.dayReachedExpert ?? 'Never'}
                    </li>
                    <li>
                      • Master / Hero Trust: {liveResult.metrics.masterAvgTrust} /{' '}
                      {liveResult.metrics.heroAvgTrust}
                    </li>
                    <li>
                      • Traps Triggered / Mass Bypassed / Sulphur Failures:{' '}
                      {liveResult.metrics.trapTriggeredCount} /{' '}
                      {liveResult.metrics.trapBypassedMassCount} /{' '}
                      {liveResult.metrics.reactionFailureSulphurCount}
                    </li>
                  </ul>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-2">
                    Notable Campaign Chronicles
                  </div>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
                    {liveResult.metrics.notableStoryLog.map((line, idx) => (
                      <li key={idx}>• {line}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==== BEGIN FILE src/utils/sound.ts BLOB 78007073304f450a92bc29041686faec0137f49b ====
// Procedural Web Audio Synthesizer for Trap & Tonic: Conflict of Interest
class SoundEngine {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  public muted: boolean = false;

  private getContext(): AudioContext | null {
    if (this.muted || typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    if (!this.ctx) {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
      if (AudioCtx) {
        this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
      }
    }
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }

  public playCoin(): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'triangle';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1180, now);
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1580, now + 0.055);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.14, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.24);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.25);
  }

  public playBrew(): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    [320, 440, 587].forEach((freq, i) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + i * 0.045);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 1.15, now + i * 0.045 + 0.14);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.11, now + i * 0.045);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + i * 0.045 + 0.16);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(now + i * 0.045);
      osc.stop(now + i * 0.045 + 0.17);
    });
  }

  public playRatchet(): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sawtooth';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(210, now);
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(140, now + 0.04);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.11);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.12);
  }

  public playImpact(heavy: boolean = false): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = heavy ? 'sawtooth' : 'square';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(heavy ? 110 : 190, now);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(32, now + (heavy ? 0.24 : 0.12));
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(heavy ? 0.22 : 0.12, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + (heavy ? 0.25 : 0.13));
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + (heavy ? 0.26 : 0.14));
  }

  public playReactionFizz(): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sawtooth';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(640, now);
    osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(240, now + 0.22);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.23);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.24);
  }

  public playCatalystChord(): void {
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    [220, 277.18, 329.63, 440, 659.25].forEach((freq, idx) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + idx * 0.03);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.09, now + idx * 0.03);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.85);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(now + idx * 0.03);
      osc.stop(now + 0.86);
    });
  }
}

export const soundFX = new SoundEngine();

==== END FILE src/utils/sound.ts ====

const terms = { priceMultiplier:1, warrantyBacked:false, exclusiveDeal:false, secretProvenance:false };

class MemoryStorage {
  data = new Map<string,string>(); failAt = -1; writes = 0;
  getItem(k:string) { return this.data.get(k) ?? null; }
  setItem(k:string,v:string) { if (++this.writes === this.failAt) throw Error('interruption'); this.data.set(k,v); }
  removeItem(k:string) { this.data.delete(k); }
}
const fixture = (name:string) => fs.readFileSync(new URL(`./fixtures/${name}`,import.meta.url),'utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'');

  assert.equal(createInitialSaveState(123).parties[0].members[0].massKg,b.parties[0].members[0].massKg);
});

test('nested corruption rejects atomically; healthy checkpoints survive interrupted writes',()=>{
  const good=createInitialSaveState();
  for(const damage of [(s:any)=>s.parties[0].members[0]=null,(s:any)=>delete s.parties[0].members[0].massKg,
    (s:any)=>delete s.products[0].counterClasses,(s:any)=>s.scheduledMatchups.cellar.partyId='missing',
    (s:any)=>s.products[0].stock=-1,(s:any)=>s.version='99',(s:any)=>s.skills[0].costPoints=0,(s:any)=>s.debt=Infinity]) {
    const bad=structuredClone(good);damage(bad); const st=new MemoryStorage();
    st.setItem(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(bad));st.setItem(SAVE_BACKUP_KEY,JSON.stringify(good));
    assert.equal(validateSavePayload(JSON.stringify(bad)).valid,false);
    assert.equal(persistAtomicSaveSafe(bad,st as any),false);assert.equal(st.getItem(SAVE_BACKUP_KEY),JSON.stringify(good));
    assert.equal(loadGameWithRecovery(st as any).source,'BACKUP');
  }
  for(const failAt of [1,2]) {
    const st=new MemoryStorage();st.data.set(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(good));st.failAt=failAt;
    assert.equal(persistAtomicSaveSafe({...good,gold:1000},st as any),false);
    assert.equal(loadGameWithRecovery(st as any).state.gold,good.gold);
  }
  const st=new MemoryStorage();st.setItem(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY,'bad');assert.equal(loadGameWithRecovery(st as any).writeBlocked,true);assert.equal(st.getItem(SAVE_STORAGE_KEY),'bad');
});

import fs from 'node:fs';

const out=process.env.EVIDENCE_DIR ?? 'artifacts/sim002b';fs.mkdirSync(out,{recursive:true});
const url=process.env.TEST_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:4178';
const executablePath=process.env.BROWSER_PATH || undefined;
const browser=await chromium.launch({headless:true,executablePath});
const evidence:any[]=[];const errors:any[]=[];
async function boot(name:string,keys:Record<string,string>={},mobile?:{width:number;height:number}) {
  const c=await browser.newContext({viewport:mobile??{width:1440,height:1000},isMobile:!!mobile,hasTouch:!!mobile});
  await c.addInitScript(data=>{if(!sessionStorage.getItem('__boot')){for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(data))localStorage.setItem(k,v);sessionStorage.setItem('__boot','1');}},keys);
  const p=await c.newPage();p.on('pageerror',e=>errors.push({name,error:e.message}));p.on('console',m=>{if(m.type()==='error')evidence.push({name,console:m.text()});});
  await p.goto(url);const open=p.getByRole('button',{name:/^(Open Merchant Ledger|Continue Ledger)/});if(mobile)await open.tap();else await open.click();
  await p.waitForTimeout(80);return {c,p};
}
