      const after={x:await canvas.getAttribute('data-camera-x'),y:await canvas.getAttribute('data-camera-y'),scroll:await p.locator('main').evaluate(el=>el.scrollLeft)};
      assert.notEqual(before.x,after.x);assert.equal(before.scroll,0);assert.equal(after.scroll,0);await shot(p,`${name}-${scale}-panned`);
      const geo=await p.locator('main').evaluate(el=>({width:el.clientWidth,scrollWidth:el.scrollWidth,controls:[...el.querySelectorAll('button,select')].map(e=>({text:e.textContent?.trim(),x:e.getBoundingClientRect().x,right:e.getBoundingClientRect().right,scroll:e.scrollWidth,width:e.clientWidth}))}));
      assert.equal(geo.scrollWidth,geo.width);assert(geo.controls.every(e=>e.x>=0 && e.right<=viewport.width+1));
      evidence.push({name,scale,before,after,trade,geometry:geo});
    }
    await c.close();
  }
  assert.deepEqual(errors,[]);console.log('Browser recovery, import, conservation, temporal reset, portrait touch: assertions passed');
} finally {fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'browser-foundation.json'),JSON.stringify({evidence,errors},null,2));await browser.close();}

==== END FILE scripts/browser_foundation.ts ====

==== BEGIN FILE scripts/replay_parity.ts BLOB 25560f91739bcedf12984b253dc282db61f0bab0 ====
/** Builder verification using real stock-card clicks; historical fixtures are labelled diagnostics. */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import zlib from 'node:zlib';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { chromium } from 'playwright';
import { normalizeLoadedSave } from '../src/engine/saveManager';
import { computeQuotation, executeCommercialTransaction } from '../src/engine/economyEngine';

const out=process.env.EVIDENCE_DIR ?? 'artifacts/sim002b'; fs.mkdirSync(out,{recursive:true});
const base=process.env.TEST_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:4178';
const browser=await chromium.launch({headless:true, executablePath:process.env.BROWSER_PATH || undefined});
const context=await browser.newContext({viewport:{width:1440,height:1000}});
const page=await context.newPage(); const errors:string[]=[];
page.on('pageerror',e=>errors.push(e.message));
await page.addInitScript(()=>{const pending=sessionStorage.getItem('__fixture');if(pending){localStorage.clear();localStorage.setItem('trap_and_tonic_save_v2',pending);sessionStorage.removeItem('__fixture');}});
await page.goto(base);
const suites=process.argv.includes('--quick') ? ['historical-f368-60'] : ['historical-f368-60','historical-c45-60','captured-ui-60'];
const summaries:any[]=[];
try {
  for(const suite of suites) {
    const cases=JSON.parse(zlib.gunzipSync(fs.readFileSync(new URL(`./fixtures/${suite}.json.gz`,import.meta.url))).toString());
    const records:any[]=[];
    for(const [i,t] of cases.entries()) {
      let state=normalizeLoadedSave(t.before);
      if(t.commands.length) state.tradeTerms=t.commands[0].terms;
      await page.evaluate(s=>sessionStorage.setItem('__fixture',JSON.stringify(s)),state);
      await page.reload();await page.getByRole('button',{name:/^(Open Merchant Ledger|Continue Ledger)/}).click();
      await page.waitForTimeout(30);
      const steps:any[]=[];
      for(const action of t.commands) {
        const before=structuredClone(state), p=state.products.find(p=>p.id===action.productId)!;
        const buyer=(action.buyerType==='MASTER'?state.masters:state.parties).find(b=>b.id===action.buyerId)!;
        // Historical traces target the scheduled customers. Selecting also exercises the real UI control.
        const select=page.getByRole('combobox',{name:action.buyerType==='MASTER'?'Scheduled Dungeon Master':'Scheduled Adventuring Party'});
        if(await select.inputValue()!==buyer.id) {await select.selectOption(buyer.id);state.scheduledMatchups[state.activeRegion][action.buyerType==='MASTER'?'masterId':'partyId']=buyer.id;}
        const quote=computeQuotation(p,buyer,action.buyerType,{...action.terms,day:state.day,seed:state.seed,rulesVersion:state.rulesVersion,unlockedSkills:state.skills.filter(s=>s.unlocked).map(s=>s.effectKey)});
        const tx=executeCommercialTransaction(state,action.buyerType,buyer.id,p,action.terms);
        const title=p.name+' [#'+(p.formulaSignature||p.id.slice(-6)).toUpperCase()+']';
        const card=page.getByText(title,{exact:true}).locator('../..');
        const button=card.getByRole('button').first();const quoteCard=await card.innerText();
        const enabled=await button.isEnabled();
        assert.equal(enabled,tx.success,`${suite}/${i}: UI acceptance differs: ${tx.reason}`);
        if(enabled) {
          if(process.argv.includes('--negative')) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'negative-quote.json'),JSON.stringify({actualExpected:quote.unitPrice,mutatedExpected:quote.unitPrice+1,button:await button.innerText()}));
          assert.match(await button.innerText(),new RegExp(`\\(${quote.unitPrice+(process.argv.includes('--negative')?1:0)}g\\)`));
          await button.click(); state=tx.nextState!;
        } else { assert(!quote.acceptable);assert(quote.refusalReason);assert.equal(tx.reason,quote.refusalReason);assert(quoteCard.includes(quote.refusalReason)); }
        await page.waitForTimeout(20);
        const actual=await page.evaluate(()=>JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('trap_and_tonic_save_v2')!));
        let diff:string|null=null;try{assert.deepEqual(actual,state);}catch(e){diff=String(e);}
        steps.push({action,before,quote,quoteCard,enabled,expected:tx,actual,diff});
        if(diff) { fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'parity-failure.json'),JSON.stringify(steps));assert.fail(`${suite}/${i}: ${diff}`); }
      }
      records.push({index:i,strategy:t.strategy,day:t.day,steps,matched:true});
      if(i===0) await page.screenshot({path:path.join(out,`${suite}-desktop.png`)});
      if((i+1)%12===0) console.log(`${suite}: ${i+1}/60 matched`);
    }
    assert.equal(records.length,60);assert(records.some(r=>r.steps.length>0));
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,`${suite}-replay.json.gz`),zlib.gzipSync(JSON.stringify(records)));
    summaries.push({suite,traces:60,matched:records.length,actions:records.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.steps.length,0)});
  }
  assert.deepEqual(errors,[]);
  // Mutation changes the actual compared quote, not a precomputed PASS flag.
  const negative=process.argv.includes('--negative');
  const last=summaries.at(-1); assert(last.actions>0);
  // The per-action comparison above is the acceptance assertion; a negative run mutates its real expected quote.
  assert(!negative, 'Use --negative to mutate a real quote in the action loop');
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'parity-summary.json'),JSON.stringify({status:'SELF-TESTED / INDEPENDENT ACCEPTANCE PENDING',summaries,errors},null,2));
} finally {await browser.close();}

==== END FILE scripts/replay_parity.ts ====

  await c.setOffline(true);

  await p.goto(url);await p.getByRole('button',{name:/^Continue Ledger/}).tap();

  assert.equal(after,before);const stillBlocked=await p.evaluate(async()=>{try{await fetch('/uncached-cold-probe?'+Date.now());return false;}catch{return true;}});assert(stillBlocked);
  await p.screenshot({path:path.join(out,'offline-cold-reopen.png')});assert.deepEqual(errors,[]);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'offline-proof.json'),JSON.stringify({url,profile,networkBlockedBeforeClose:blocked,networkBlockedAfterColdOpen:stillBlocked,before:JSON.parse(before!),after:JSON.parse(after!),errors,physicalAndroid:'NOT TESTED',apk:'NOT TESTED',osKill:'NOT TESTED'},null,2));
  console.log('Network-restricted production browser cold reopen: exact save preserved');
} finally {await c.close();}

==== BEGIN FILE scripts/run_sim_002b.ts BLOB 2a290e429df15819957f06af200cad6d3260e9ac ====
/** New evidence only. Never writes SIM-001 or previous SIM-002 results. */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import zlib from 'node:zlib';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { runSingleCampaign, StrategyId, CampaignMetrics } from '../src/engine/campaignRunner';
import { validateSavePayload } from '../src/engine/saveManager';
const out=process.env.CAMPAIGN_DIR ?? 'artifacts/sim002b/campaigns';
assert(!fs.existsSync(path.join(out,'campaign_results.csv')),'Existing evidence must not be overwritten');fs.mkdirSync(out,{recursive:true});
const git=(...args:string[])=>execFileSync('git',args,{encoding:'utf8'}).trim();
assert.equal(git('diff','--name-only','c45f33e143f71acd7a66edacf773161fb4b9416f','--','sim_001_archive','public/sim_001'),'','Frozen SIM-001 changed');
const sourceCommit=git('rev-parse','HEAD'),sourceTree=git('rev-parse','HEAD^{tree}');
const strategies: StrategyId[]=['BALANCED_MERCHANT','DUNGEON_LOYALIST','HERO_BENEFACTOR','ALCHEMICAL_INDUSTRIALIST','PREDATORY_CAPITALIST'];
const metrics:CampaignMetrics[]=[];const sampleEvents:any[]=[];const sampleActions:any[]=[];
for(let offset=0;offset<1000;offset++){
  const seed=20260001+offset;
  for(const strategy of strategies){
    const result=runSingleCampaign(seed,strategy,120,offset<5,offset===0?event=>{if(event.before.day<=3)sampleActions.push({strategy,...event});}:undefined);
    const validation=validateSavePayload(JSON.stringify(result.finalState));assert(validation.valid,`${seed}/${strategy}: ${validation.error}`);
    assert.equal(result.finalState.financialLedger.length,120);
    assert.equal(result.metrics.grossRevenue,result.finalState.contractHistory.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.totalRevenue,0));
    assert.equal(result.metrics.endingCash,result.finalState.financialLedger[0].endingCash);
    metrics.push(result.metrics);sampleEvents.push(...result.events);
  }
  if((offset+1)%100===0)console.log(`${(offset+1)*5}/5000 campaigns complete`);
}
const columns=Object.keys(metrics[0]) as (keyof CampaignMetrics)[];const csv=(v:any)=>JSON.stringify(typeof v==='object'?JSON.stringify(v):String(v??''));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'campaign_results.csv'),columns.join(',')+'\n'+metrics.map(m=>columns.map(k=>csv(m[k])).join(',')).join('\n')+'\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'transaction-replays.json.gz'),zlib.gzipSync(JSON.stringify(sampleActions)));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'economy-events.json'),JSON.stringify(sampleEvents));
const median=(xs:number[])=>{xs.sort((a,b)=>a-b);return (xs[499]+xs[500])/2;};
const summary=strategies.map(strategy=>{const rows=metrics.filter(m=>m.strategy===strategy);return {strategy,campaigns:rows.length,medianRevenue:median(rows.map(m=>m.grossRevenue)),medianNetProfit:median(rows.map(m=>m.netProfit)),bankruptcies:rows.filter(m=>m.strictBankruptcyOccurred).length,expertUnlocked:rows.filter(m=>m.dayReachedExpert!==null).length};});
const manifest={status:'SELF-TESTED / INDEPENDENT ACCEPTANCE PENDING',sourceCommit,sourceTree,pairedSeeds:1000,campaigns:5000,horizonDays:120,node:process.version,summary,
  caveats:['Strategy policies select crafting, financing and progression timing; these campaigns do not certify whole-campaign UI equivalence.','Specialists cannot unlock Expert through casual peer codes; the bilateral solo commission still applies.','No balancing changes or independent enjoyment acceptance inferred.','Physical Android / APK / OS kill NOT TESTED.']};
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'manifest.json'),JSON.stringify(manifest,null,2));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'SIM-002B_REPORT.md'),`# SIM-002B builder campaign results\n\nSELF-TESTED / INDEPENDENT ACCEPTANCE PENDING\n\nCommit: ${sourceCommit}\nTree: ${sourceTree}\n\n1,000 identical seeds across five strategies, 120 days each. All final states validated; revenue reconciled to actual contracts and ending cash to the daily ledger.\n\n| Strategy | Median revenue | Median profit | Insolvent campaigns | Expert reached |\n|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n${summary.map(s=>`| ${s.strategy} | ${s.medianRevenue} | ${s.medianNetProfit} | ${s.bankruptcies}/1000 | ${s.expertUnlocked}/1000 |`).join('\n')}\n\n${manifest.caveats.map(c=>'- '+c).join('\n')}\n`);
const sums=fs.readdirSync(out).filter(n=>fs.statSync(path.join(out,n)).isFile()).sort().map(n=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(path.join(out,n))).digest('hex')+'  '+n).join('\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out,'SHA256SUMS.txt'),sums+'\n');console.log(JSON.stringify(manifest,null,2));

==== END FILE scripts/run_sim_002b.ts ====

      - run: npm run test:browser && npm run test:negative && npm run test:offline
